# 95-agent Phistory integration audit

Current verified runtime coverage is **87/95: 12 existing integrations +75 newly verified targets**. Each new target has a real Linux primary-request capture and an independent fresh capture. The final95-row index contains **75 Verified runtime,12 Existing runtime and8 Source archive** records. Source-file archival separately covers all83 new targets, with366 original upstream files and pinned hash evidence.

The runtime claim is the **complete request actually emitted by each explicitly named native entry point**, including its system/developer/user content and native tool schemas or text/structured action protocol. It does not mean every project role, model/provider route, later pipeline stage or optional integration was captured. LibreChat's selected Agent legitimately emits no system/developer content because no instructions were configured; no universal system was added. Orkas preserves all20 initially emitted Commander tool schemas, while later tool_load groups remain lazy. Original Linux raw traces retain native paths, IDs and times; narrow normalization is used only to compare independent runs.

**Dyad completes this87/95 milestone.** [Initial and independent fresh44153876152](https://github.com/DragonnZhang/phistory/actions/runs/44153876152) use the real production1.24.0-beta.1 package, Electron40.0.0/Chrome144.0.7559.60 and the configured custom gpt-4.1 native Basic Agent. The **complete primary raw request bodies are equal**, including all29 tool definitions, system and user content. System length is15,866 after outer-whitespace trimming,15,868 in the untouched raw string. Identical runtime sidecars confirm both actual application/browser versions. The production inspector fuses, real preload/IPC, imported Git/SQLite workspace and native application authorization remained intact; renderer CDP attached only to the application's own Electron.

The remaining **8 Source archive** projects have pinned source evidence and concrete contracts below, without a verified complete primary runtime request:

| Project | Remaining native boundary |
| --- | --- |
| [Eigent](#eigent-native-local-brain-task-api) | Real Brain auth/workforce startup includes pre-inference browser preparation; browser capture scope remains required. |
| [Eliza](#eliza-native-headless-production-message-loop) | Published production message-loop/plugin/PGlite bootstrap is identified; complete native Linux primary capture remains unverified. |
| [AIHawk](#aihawk-current-native-form-application-agent) | Actual form agent requires its real Firefox/MCP browser path and applicable login state. |
| [Open-SWE / Robin](#open-swe--robin-native-managed-runtime) | The real backend-planned authenticated managed runtime/catalog bootstrap remains unresolved; historical graph entry is not the current agent. |
| [RobotUse](#robotuse-native-simulated-robotics-episode) | Genuine RoboLab/Isaac Sim episode requires its actual GPU simulation resources. |
| [Skyvern](#skyvern-genuine-embedded-browser-agent-task) | Native embedded browser task requires real page/browser execution within the agreed capture scope. |
| [Jev-social](#jev-social-native-decision-service-protocol-boundary) | Its real decision API does not transmit the remote service-owned complete LLM system; no substitute prompt/schema is invented. |
| [Agent-QA](#agent-qa-native-configured-openai-web-run) | The configured web-run surface requires actual browser execution within the agreed capture scope. |

The verification milestones below are historical progress records. Their earlier pending states are superseded by the final index and the87/95 result above; detailed failed diagnostics remain available for provenance.

**Earlier86/95 milestone: LibreChat.** Its [initial and fresh44153052159](https://github.com/DragonnZhang/phistory/actions/runs/44153052159) use the real Mongo-backed application, native registration/login/JWT, tools catalogue, normal Agent creation and /api/agents/chat after /readyz. The configured native gpt-6.1-sol Agent has **zero system/developer content**, because no instructions were entered and LibreChat has no universal built-in system to supply. Its single complete calculator tool definition and user message are identical. Complete primary-body diff has only the top-level **user** field changing to the fresh instance's actual registered Mongo user ID. The separate earlier zero-tool request remains auxiliary and is not the selected primary. Native User-Agent parsing, ban middleware and authentication remain enabled; the real control HTTP requests carry their normal required User-Agent. Dyad was still awaiting its independent fresh validation at that milestone; it is now verified above.

**Earlier85/95 milestone: Orkas.** Its [initial and fresh44153049669](https://github.com/DragonnZhang/phistory/actions/runs/44153049669) use the real2026.11.16 application, Electron42.11.18 and native Node24.19.0, source3163c7ef1c2b03e21e6f85c6301515609361c570. Configured custom gpt-4.1 emits a42,959-character Commander system, **20 identical complete tool schemas** and identical user content. Full raw-system diff contains exactly four changed lines: three advertised Agent-root paths and the tool cwd/default-write path. All differences are the actual temporary HOME prefix and native random32-character local uid; replacing only those two fields makes the entire system equal. All other raw body fields also match. This verifies the complete **initially emitted Commander schema surface**, while tool_load's later lazy groups remain unexpanded. Dyad and LibreChat were still pending at that85/95 milestone.

**Earlier84/95 milestone: VibeSDK.** Its [initial and fresh44151053897](https://github.com/DragonnZhang/phistory/actions/runs/44151053897) preserve native google-ai-studio/gemini-3-flash-preview, an identical30,011-character system and15 identical native tools. This is the named **General first-agentic** task through real Vite Workers, local D1/KV/R2/Durable Objects, CSRF registration/JWT and WebSocket generate_all; the default Think/phasic pipeline is a separate surface. User content differs only in the two native random ProjectName suffixes; complete-request byte equality is not claimed.

**Earlier83/95 milestone: QM and AgentLink passed independent Linux validation.** QM's [initial and fresh44149825515](https://github.com/DragonnZhang/phistory/actions/runs/44149825515) use native gpt-6.1-sol, zero system characters, an identical13,926-character developer message,20 identical tool definitions and equal task user content. This is the genuine qm init/up Docker/PostgreSQL/local-sandbox stack with signed one-time admin login, actual cookie and /api/turn; PUBLIC_API_URL normally points at the real core container DNS on8080. AgentLink's [initial and fresh44149918404](https://github.com/DragonnZhang/phistory/actions/runs/44149918404) use native gpt-5.6-sol,28 identical tools and equal user content; the5314-character system differs only in the temporary Project root path. It runs the source-built TUI and WorkspaceHost through a real PTY/Enter task, with normal Ctrl+C cancellation/exit. The earlier PTY shutdown/export failures below are resolved, not a remaining blocker.

**Earlier81/95 milestone:** three further primary surfaces passed independent Linux validation: **DarkMoon** [initial and fresh44148251285](https://github.com/DragonnZhang/phistory/actions/runs/44148251285), with identical118,997-character system,23 tool definitions including all13 darkmoon_* MCP tools, and identical32-character user content; **LoopTroop** [initial44148255867](https://github.com/DragonnZhang/phistory/actions/runs/44148255867) and [fresh44148945773](https://github.com/DragonnZhang/phistory/actions/runs/44148945773), with native gpt-4.1, identical4158-character relevant-files task user prompt and four identical glob/grep/question/read tools, while its13,734-character system differs only in actual HOME/worktree/AGENTS paths; and **AutoGPT** [initial and fresh44148244880](https://github.com/DragonnZhang/phistory/actions/runs/44148244880), with native anthropic/claude-sonnet-5-5, identical29,900-character system and20 identical tools. LoopTroop verifies its initial PROM0 scan, not the later interview. DarkMoon's real MCP/privacy source fingerprints and published image identities passed; AutoGPT's official v0.11.3 appliance is pinned to image sha256:6dd38566729c0e76863960f57018191ab9f49b80a947fa26a6ebd2d200507e23, OCI revision657153887ea25aca92014ee713f9ceb344378e74. AgentLink was still pending at this earlier milestone; its later verified result is recorded above.

- **Agentlas:** [initial44143590169](https://github.com/DragonnZhang/phistory/actions/runs/44143590169) and [fresh44145949599](https://github.com/DragonnZhang/phistory/actions/runs/44145949599), actual Claude2.1.326/Opus5.5, activated user skill2908characters and **53 identical tool definitions including real Agentlas MCP tools**. The321-character system differs only in the actual current minute,6:15→6:17. This is the genuine workspace @agent task.

AgentLink was still pending at this earlier milestone; its later verified result is recorded above.

Hivekeep's actual Queenie onboarding passed [initial Linux capture](https://github.com/DragonnZhang/phistory/actions/runs/44136474089) and [independent fresh Linux capture](https://github.com/DragonnZhang/phistory/actions/runs/44137411719). Both preserve **qwen/qwen4-max**, an identical **53,564-character system** and **76 identical native tool definitions**, including the unchanged configurator, browser, contacts and email registrations. The native generic OpenAI-compatible connector authenticated and selected its own model from the real public OpenRouter catalogue. The original catalogue bytes and SHA-256 are archived beside the trace; only model POSTs use the owned capture-only transport, with the native body unchanged. This is the complete initial Queenie configuration surface, not every user-created agent or a validated paid OpenRouter credential.

Independent Linux validation now also covers [LongHorizon's initial Codex manager](https://github.com/DragonnZhang/phistory/actions/runs/44129910246) and [DB-GPT's complete official ReAct example plus UFO's native LinuxAgent](https://github.com/DragonnZhang/phistory/actions/runs/44130213522). LongHorizon uses its actual Codex host, native gpt-5.6-sol and nine identical tool definitions; its 22,742-character developer message differs only in isolated HOME. Its outer manager instructions remain in the actual user message. DB-GPT preserves its native Qwen2.5-Coder-32B-Instruct and 3,576-character system, differing only in current time. UFO preserves its native gpt-4o and identical 16,840-character LinuxAgent system, with actual authenticated device/Bash MCP services. DB-GPT and UFO natively express actions in text rather than JSON function tools. Their raw startup probes and synthetic embedding responses remain in trace evidence; only the actual primary request is selected. These named first-request surfaces do not claim later executor/auditor stages, full database application operation or Windows GUI agents.

Runtime snapshots and original source-file archives remain separate evidence types. A source archive is not an emitted API request; current runtime support is determined by the final87/95 result and the named-entry index below.

Latest independent [fresh validation for WeChat's portable reply engine and Agent-S S3](https://github.com/DragonnZhang/phistory/actions/runs/44122341424) passed. WeChat preserves its native 84-character persona system and all 13 tool definitions exactly. Agent-S uses its actual Linux X display and native gpt-5-2025-08-07; the entire initial request body, including screenshot, is identical across the independent runs. Its 11,468-character system carries the native Python action protocol with zero JSON tools. These are the portable reply and S3 first-action surfaces; full macOS WeChat operation and experimental S4 remain separate.

Latest independent [fresh validation for Web3GPT, Ouroboros and Markus](https://github.com/DragonnZhang/phistory/actions/runs/44119483299) passed. Web3GPT has native model gpt-6.1-terra, an identical 1,176-character developer message and three identical tools. Ouroboros has 164 identical tool definitions and native main systems of 721,779/721,762 characters, differing in actual startup paths, times, session/project IDs and identity-map revision. Markus is the confirmed native Secretary with about 40,743 system characters and 32 tools; only shell_execute's native HOME/AgentID/workspace description varies, and its tool definitions match after that narrowly scoped path normalization. Raw traces retain all original values.

Latest independent [fresh validation for AutoRef, the critic-training actor and public SoulForge v2/Empryo](https://github.com/DragonnZhang/phistory/actions/runs/44117783483) passed. AutoRef's merged initial and fresh **complete raw request dictionaries are identical**, including the real input PNG, user-only native scene prompt, model gpt-5.5-2026-04-23 and zero JSON tools. The critic actor preserves its 530-character system and native zero-JSON-tool protocol; SoulForge preserves eight tools and its 14,945-character system differs only in workdir.

Earlier independent [fresh validation for Hive, IntentKit Lead, MIRIX, Atomic Agent and Agent Zero](https://github.com/DragonnZhang/phistory/actions/runs/44116259763) passed after inspecting their actual primary requests. Hive has 23 tools and identical system; Lead has 13 tools with only chat_id variation; MIRIX has nine canonically sorted tool definitions with only Current Time variation; Atomic has 92 tools with only isolated HOME/workspace differences; Agent Zero's native Manual preset has a 37,668-character main system, zero JSON tools and model `openai/gpt-6.1-sol`, with equal system across captures. Raw values remain unchanged.

Latest verification includes [Raven and ZCode](https://github.com/DragonnZhang/phistory/actions/runs/44103166713), [CowAgent](https://github.com/DragonnZhang/phistory/actions/runs/44102837808), and [BambooAI and AgenticSeek](https://github.com/DragonnZhang/phistory/actions/runs/44104927439). Native tool counts are 32, 24 and 18 for the first three; BambooAI and AgenticSeek use their native text/code protocols with zero JSON tools. CowAgent's only tool-definition variation is the actual isolated workspace path in its bash description; raw values are retained. Its default memory is native keyword search when no embedding provider is configured. The chronological sections below retain earlier milestones and initial source-only hypotheses; current runtime support is determined by these verified captures and the registry's latest policy.

The survey names 95 repositories, including terminal CLIs, configurable applications, research scripts, and outer orchestration systems. The starting Phistory registry already covers 12; these are skipped. The other 83 belong in a separately identified source archive immediately, with request-capture drivers added where a reproducible entry point is available. A source snapshot contains real upstream file bytes, not an emitted API request. It must not be labeled `trace.jsonl` or described as a resolved runtime system prompt/toolset.

The table distinguishes the initial registry overlap from new source targets. “CLI candidate” means an official executable/source entry exists; it does not claim a Linux capture succeeded. The exact commits and content hashes belong to `phistory/source_catalog.json`. Source references below point to pinned upstream evidence, and README references identify the official installation/entry documentation.

## Confirmed CLI contracts

Runtime status after Linux runs [44078434491](https://github.com/DragonnZhang/phistory/actions/runs/44078434491) and [44081049485](https://github.com/DragonnZhang/phistory/actions/runs/44081049485): Gemini plus Goose, Crush, mini-swe-agent, nanobot and Bub now have real request captures, bringing this survey to 18 runtime integrations including the original 12. The five latter agents passed independent fresh-home/cache smoke captures with exactly equal canonical tool definitions (17, 26, 1, 22 and 18 tools respectively). Crush, mini and nanobot also have identical normalized prompt Markdown; Goose differs only in its current-time field and Bub only in its Date field. Raw Linux traces retain these values. Jcode's first attempt failed during release-layout installation, before a model request; its launcher/sidecar bundle fix requires another CI attempt. Registry entries for the next eight are implementation candidates until corresponding Linux captures succeed.

| Project | Installation source | Normal single-task entry | Capture condition |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Gemini CLI | npm `@google/gemini-cli` | `gemini -p "Reply with one short sentence."` | Use a fake `GEMINI_API_KEY` behind claude-tap; preserve native Google request bodies. `GOOGLE_GEMINI_BASE_URL` changes auth selection to GATEWAY in current source, so record it if selected. [README](https://github.com/google-gemini/gemini-cli/blob/main/README.md), [content generator](https://github.com/google-gemini/gemini-cli/blob/main/packages/core/src/core/contentGenerator.ts) |
| Goose | official `aaif-goose/goose` release binary | `goose run --text "Reply with one short sentence." --no-session` | `GOOSE_PROVIDER`/`GOOSE_MODEL` and extension config select the surface; retain the unpinned default lane. [CLI definitions](https://github.com/aaif-goose/goose/blob/main/crates/goose-cli/src/cli.rs) |
| Crush | npm `@charmland/crush` or official release | `crush run "Reply with one short sentence."` | Isolate config/provider catalog; OpenAI-compatible and native provider paths differ. [README](https://github.com/charmbracelet/crush/blob/main/README.md), [run command](https://github.com/charmbracelet/crush/blob/main/internal/cmd/run.go) |
| Letta Code | npm `@letta-ai/letta-code` | `letta -p "Reply with one short sentence."` | Cloud is the documented default. `--backend local` must be a separate named surface; a local prompt does not stand in for Cloud-managed prompts. [README](https://github.com/letta-ai/letta-code/blob/main/README.md) |
| mini-swe-agent | PyPI `mini-swe-agent` | `mini --task "Reply with one short sentence." --yolo --exit-immediately` | Supply model/provider through isolated normal configuration. Preserve text-action/toolcall model distinctions. [CLI definitions](https://github.com/SWE-agent/mini-swe-agent/blob/main/src/minisweagent/run/mini.py) |
| Fibona | pinned source + Python 3.12/uv | `uv run fibona.py --boot-mode minimal "Reply with one short sentence."` | `FIBONA_BASE_URL`, `FIBONA_API_KEY`, `FIBONA_MODEL` configure Responses; terminal bootstrap is a distinct mode and contributes developer instructions. [README](https://github.com/FibonaAI/fibona-agent/blob/main/README.md) |
| Bub | PyPI `bub` | official `bub chat` app entry | Plugin/transport configuration and a bounded single-turn driver still need verification. [README](https://github.com/bubbuild/bub/blob/main/README.md), [package scripts](https://github.com/bubbuild/bub/blob/main/pyproject.toml) |
| nanobot | PyPI `nanobot-ai` | official `nanobot agent` entry | Current config/CLI layout has changed; verify current single-message flags and selected local gateway before a CI driver is declared supported. [README](https://github.com/HKUDS/nanobot/blob/main/README.md), [CLI reference](https://github.com/HKUDS/nanobot/blob/main/docs/cli-reference.md) |
| Jcode | official Rust source/release binary | command flags pending driver validation | Do not infer an npm package from its name. Archive base prompt plus tool registration, then validate official headless/provider config in Linux. [README](https://github.com/1jehuang/jcode/blob/master/README.md), [Cargo manifest](https://github.com/1jehuang/jcode/blob/master/Cargo.toml) |

Grok's npm archive README names `@xai-official/grok`, links to `x.ai/cli` and `docs.x.ai/build/overview`, and describes the same `grok` command as `xai-org/grok-build`; it is an existing integration. MiMo's npm registry repository field directly identifies `XiaomiMiMo/MiMo-Code`. [Grok source README](https://github.com/xai-org/grok-build/blob/main/README.md), [Grok package archive](https://registry.npmjs.org/@xai-official/grok/-/grok-1.0.88.tgz), [MiMo package metadata](https://registry.npmjs.org/@mimo-ai%2Fcli/latest).

## Source-surface repairs

The initial survey citations often selected a short discriminating fragment rather than the full prompt assembly surface. Source archival therefore includes the following additional pinned upstream files:

- Goose: base `prompts/system.md`, subagent template, prompt manager and template registry, alongside the developer extension. Extension instructions are not the whole system prompt.
- Agent Zero: the root `agent.system*.md` family, their small Python template providers, and plugin system/tool prompt templates. The solving section alone omits identity, environment, communication, skills and tool instructions.
- Fibona: `core/agent.py` carries both `INSTRUCTIONS` and the `SET_NEXT_CELL` schema. Terminal `context.py` adds developer-role working instructions; minimal bootstrap is separate.
- Prime Agent: core/usage/opinionated/model layers, system-prompt assembler and the native `ipython_tool_schema` implementation. REPL programmatic tool guidance and API tool schema are distinct artifacts.
- AutoResearchClaw: MCP tool schema registry and code-agent adapter, with explicit external-CLI boundaries.
- RobotUse: native tool registry, schema and discovery code; physical/simulator environments remain a runtime condition.
- TrashClaw: the same source file contains both prompt assembly and the `TOOLS` declarations.
- Skyvern: action model definitions and action types accompany action prompt templates; these are the actual structured action surface, not a generic native function-tool claim.

LibreChat and DeepAnalyze do not have a universal built-in system prompt to export for every deployment. Archive their configuration/assembly code and preserve that distinction. Likewise, external CLI tools used by LongHorizon, Dark-Moon, LoopTroop or AutoRef are not declared to be the outer project's own universal toolset.

All real execution captures and fresh smoke runs belong in Linux GitHub Actions. Source-file download, hashing, rendering and fake-CLI tests can run locally; neither an installable package nor a checked source hash is evidence that a runtime request capture passed.

## Appendix: next Linux request-capture contracts

These contracts were checked against the published release/package source, without executing the real CLIs locally. They are inputs for Linux CI validation, not claims that those captures have passed. Keep the existing isolated HOME/XDG setup and fake credentials. In each command below, the CLI is the child of `claude-tap run <executable> --mode forward --export-prompt ... --no-yolo -- ...`; no command should be launched directly against a real model service.

### Shared claude-tap protocol requirement

At the pinned tap revision, `clients.generic(cmd)` advertises only `PASSTHROUGH`. Request export can later infer the provider from the raw request, but dummy-response generation and streaming detection select their behavior from the already chosen `Protocol`. Consequently, a generic Chat Completions or Anthropic child currently receives a Responses-shaped fallback dummy and may retry or hang.

The shared correction is to give generic clients `(ANTHROPIC, OPENAI, GEMINI, PASSTHROUGH)`, in that order with catch-all last. This reuses typed path selection, JSON/SSE dummy generation and streaming detection in both forward and reverse transports. Six duplicate client definitions are unnecessary. Native `/v1/messages`, `/v1/responses`, `/v1/chat/completions` and Google model paths are covered; the existing OpenAI `suffix:/chat/completions` rule also covers OpenRouter's `/api/v1/chat/completions`. Startup GETs and arbitrary gateway paths remain separate concerns: catch-all dummy output is not a model catalog/auth bootstrap implementation. [Generic client](https://github.com/DragonnZhang/claude-tap/blob/64872f527b862906b5944e74b4d155e6cbb4bd44/claude_tap/clients.py), [protocol selection](https://github.com/DragonnZhang/claude-tap/blob/64872f527b862906b5944e74b4d155e6cbb4bd44/claude_tap/protocols.py), [capture dummy responses](https://github.com/DragonnZhang/claude-tap/blob/64872f527b862906b5944e74b4d155e6cbb4bd44/claude_tap/pipeline.py).

### Goose 1.59.2

Use the official release asset `goose-x86_64-unknown-linux-gnu.tar.gz` from tag `v1.59.2`; the executable is `goose`. A fresh profile has no universal configured provider/model, and no enabled extension set is inferred merely from the presence of a default-extension constant. [Release](https://github.com/aaif-goose/goose/releases/tag/v1.59.2), [session resolution](https://github.com/aaif-goose/goose/blob/v1.59.2/crates/goose-cli/src/session/builder.rs), [CLI arguments](https://github.com/aaif-goose/goose/blob/v1.59.2/crates/goose-cli/src/cli.rs).

Set `GOOSE_PATH_ROOT` to an absolute isolated directory and `GOOSE_DISABLE_KEYRING=1`. Goose then reads `<GOOSE_PATH_ROOT>/config/config.yaml`. Set `OPENAI_API_KEY=phistory-fake-api-key`. The current configuration wizard enables the in-process Developer extension as `type: platform`, not the older bundled-MCP `builtin` form:

```yaml
extensions:
  developer:
    enabled: true
    type: platform
    name: developer
    description: Developer extension
    display_name: Developer
    bundled: true
```

```text
goose run --provider openai --text "Reply with one short sentence." --no-session
```

With a CLI provider selection and no model selection, the session builder uses that provider's own metadata default, currently OpenAI `gpt-6.1-sol`. Setting only environment `GOOSE_PROVIDER=openai` does not enter that fallback branch: the unflagged path also requires a configured `GOOSE_MODEL`. If the default Phistory lane uses an unflagged command, put `GOOSE_PROVIDER: openai` and `GOOSE_MODEL: gpt-6.1-sol` in its isolated YAML and record the fixed capture configuration; do not describe it as a universal first-run model. The developer-extension configuration is also part of the recorded surface. [Provider default and request routing](https://github.com/aaif-goose/goose/blob/v1.59.2/crates/goose-providers/src/openai.rs), [wizard extension setup](https://github.com/aaif-goose/goose/blob/v1.59.2/crates/goose-cli/src/commands/configure.rs), [path isolation](https://github.com/aaif-goose/goose/blob/v1.59.2/crates/goose/src/config/paths.rs).

For forward TLS, Goose supports `GOOSE_CA_CERT_PATH` explicitly. Point it to tap's generated Linux CA at `<isolated XDG_DATA_HOME>/claude-tap/ca.pem` if generic CA environment variables do not suffice. The provider adds this PEM bundle to its reqwest client. Do not replace the real raw request or disable TLS verification. [TLS setting](https://github.com/aaif-goose/goose/blob/v1.59.2/crates/goose/src/config/tls.rs), [TLS application](https://github.com/aaif-goose/goose/blob/v1.59.2/crates/goose-providers/src/api_client.rs).

### Crush 0.101.0

Use npm `@charmland/crush@0.101.0` or the same official release's Linux binary. The executable is `crush`, and `crush run "Reply with one short sentence."` is the normal one-shot entry. The published npm wrapper identifies the official source repository. [Release](https://github.com/charmbracelet/crush/releases/tag/v0.101.0), [package metadata](https://registry.npmjs.org/@charmland%2Fcrush/0.101.0), [run implementation](https://github.com/charmbracelet/crush/blob/v0.101.0/internal/cmd/run.go).

The fresh install has no persisted large/small model choice. To create a repeatable fake-provider lane, set `CRUSH_GLOBAL_CONFIG` to an isolated **directory**, and write `crush.json` inside it. It is not a config-filename environment variable. Native JSON config remains supported alongside the newer `crushrc` format:

```json
{
  "models": {
    "large": {"model": "gpt-4.1", "provider": "openai"},
    "small": {"model": "gpt-4.1", "provider": "openai"}
  },
  "providers": {
    "openai": {"api_key": "phistory-fake-api-key"}
  },
  "options": {
    "disable_provider_auto_update": true,
    "disable_metrics": true
  }
}
```

Set `OPENAI_API_KEY=phistory-fake-api-key`, `CRUSH_DISABLE_PROVIDER_AUTO_UPDATE=1`, `CRUSH_DISABLE_METRICS=1`, and an isolated `CRUSH_DATA_DIR` in addition to HOME/XDG. Both model choices are explicit capture conditions, not built-in Crush defaults. The built-in `openai` provider migrates to the Responses endpoint type in this release; use its actual request body rather than converting it to Chat Completions. An empty disposable work directory also avoids the separate project-initialization flow. [Config locations and migration](https://github.com/charmbracelet/crush/blob/v0.101.0/internal/config/load.go), [model/provider/options schema](https://github.com/charmbracelet/crush/blob/v0.101.0/internal/config/config.go), [initialization](https://github.com/charmbracelet/crush/blob/v0.101.0/internal/config/init.go).

### mini-swe-agent 2.4.6

Use PyPI `mini-swe-agent==2.4.6` with executable `mini`. Read the installed package's `minisweagent/run/mini.py`, `run/utilities/config.py`, `models/__init__.py` and `config/mini.yaml`; the wheel is the release source of truth. [Release metadata](https://pypi.org/pypi/mini-swe-agent/2.4.6/json), [release wheel](https://files.pythonhosted.org/packages/a2/00/a2f454775f69f540ab529c5f5e35d672f6491f0e6edab7cb196a0d2a0e2e/mini_swe_agent-2.4.6-py3-none-any.whl).

Set these isolated capture conditions:

```text
MSWEA_CONFIGURED=true
MSWEA_GLOBAL_CONFIG_DIR=<absolute isolated config directory>
MSWEA_MODEL_NAME=openai/gpt-4.1
OPENAI_API_KEY=phistory-fake-api-key
MSWEA_COST_TRACKING=ignore_errors
```

There is no built-in default model: precedence is explicit input, model config, then `MSWEA_MODEL_NAME`, followed by an error. `MSWEA_CONFIGURED` skips the interactive first-run configuration wizard. Keep this provider/model choice in metadata rather than implying an upstream model default.

```text
mini --task "Reply with one short sentence." --yolo --exit-immediately -c mini.yaml -c agent.step_limit=1
```

`mini.yaml` is this release's real CLI default and uses a native bash tool schema. `default.yaml` is a different text-action prompt; it must be a separate named surface if captured. A bare `-c agent.step_limit=1` would replace the default config list and lose its prompt, so retain `-c mini.yaml` explicitly. The one-step budget is necessary for an export probe: tap's text dummy contains no bash action, and the default unlimited step budget would otherwise allow format-error retries indefinitely. This budget changes execution length, not the initial prompt/tool definition. `--exit-immediately` disables the final confirmation prompt. A source survey describing an older text-only mini version must not override this published version's actual native-tool default.

### nanobot 0.4.1

Use PyPI `nanobot-ai==0.4.1`, selecting its Linux x86-64 wheel on CI. The executable is `nanobot`; the current wheel's `nanobot/cli/agent.py` exposes a true one-message path:

```text
nanobot agent --message "Reply with one short sentence."
```

The message flag selects the classic Python one-shot runtime automatically; a TTY, native UI or separate gateway daemon is not required. `--classic` is needed only to request the older interactive prompt. The one-shot still constructs the real AgentLoop, synchronizes workspace templates, registers configured tools, and emits its normal model request. [Release metadata](https://pypi.org/pypi/nanobot-ai/0.4.1/json), [Linux release wheel](https://files.pythonhosted.org/packages/27/47/972e61cfb71ed6f2393f93a0034348667973f8820a938e3cfd8a06200be0/nanobot_ai-0.4.1-py3-none-manylinux_2_17_x86_64.whl).

Under isolated HOME, write `~/.nanobot/config.json` with only fake Anthropic credentials:

```json
{"providers":{"anthropic":{"apiKey":"phistory-fake-api-key"}}}
```

Leave model/provider overrides absent. Published `AgentDefaults` uses model `anthropic/claude-opus-4-5` and provider `auto`; the provider registry routes that selection to its native Anthropic backend. The fake credential can therefore preserve the package's actual default model while still preventing a real provider call through tap capture-only mode. The workspace template files contribute prompt content and must remain in the isolated capture environment. Explicit custom endpoint/provider recipes are different surfaces, not substitutes for this default.

### Bub 0.6.1

Install **`bub[app]==0.6.1`**, not plain `bub`: the release wheel's bootstrap deliberately checks its app-extra requirements before running any CLI. Python 3.12 or newer is required. The normal one-message entry is:

```text
bub run "Reply with one short sentence."
```

Set isolated `BUB_HOME`, `BUB_API_KEY=phistory-fake-api-key` (or provider-specific `BUB_OPENROUTER_API_KEY`), and `BUB_MAX_STEPS=1`. Leave `BUB_MODEL` absent to preserve the package's actual `openrouter:openrouter/free` default. The provider uses OpenRouter's Chat Completions endpoint; the generic tap correction handles its `/api/v1/chat/completions` suffix. `bub run` builds a normal CLI-channel message, loads the framework hooks, and passes it through `process_inbound`, including the real built-in prompt and tools; it is not an SDK reimplementation or an interactive `bub chat` session. [Release metadata](https://pypi.org/pypi/bub/0.6.1/json), [release wheel](https://files.pythonhosted.org/packages/2f/3c/39be7c0b0023977208aeba3e98fedb5403297df84d29cc6a361bf3af946b/bub-0.6.1-py3-none-any.whl).

### Jcode 1.0.0

Use release asset `jcode-linux-x86_64.tar.gz` from official tag `v1.0.0`. Its clap definitions provide `run` with a positional message and `--json` or `--ndjson`; the single-message implementation constructs the normal tool registry and Agent. [Release](https://github.com/1jehuang/jcode/releases/tag/v1.0.0), [CLI arguments](https://github.com/1jehuang/jcode/blob/v1.0.0/src/cli/args.rs), [single-message execution](https://github.com/1jehuang/jcode/blob/v1.0.0/src/cli/commands.rs).

The actual Linux archive entry is a 505-byte executable launcher named `jcode-linux-x86_64`, which sets `LD_LIBRARY_PATH` to its resolved directory and executes sibling `jcode-linux-x86_64.bin`. Preserve the archive bundle and symlink the command `jcode` to that launcher; copying or renaming only one file loses its sibling/library lookup. [Release archive](https://github.com/1jehuang/jcode/releases/download/v1.0.0/jcode-linux-x86_64.tar.gz).

```text
jcode --no-update --no-selfdev run --json "Reply with one short sentence."
```

Set isolated `JCODE_HOME`, `JCODE_NON_INTERACTIVE=1`, `ANTHROPIC_API_KEY=phistory-fake-api-key`, and `JCODE_RUN_AUTO_POKE=0`. Leave model and provider flags unset. `auto` is the CLI provider default; credential discovery counts a direct Anthropic API key as an available Claude backend. A directly supplied environment key avoids the separate consent flow for importing another application's stored credential. No universal fixed model should be invented: the provider/model defaults and configuration select it. Source inspection confirms the entry and credential path; the release's TLS/capability-prefetch behavior still requires its real Linux capture. [Provider initialization and direct-key handling](https://github.com/1jehuang/jcode/blob/v1.0.0/src/cli/provider_init.rs), [config defaults](https://github.com/1jehuang/jcode/blob/v1.0.0/crates/jcode-base/src/config/default_file.rs).

## Next eight Linux runtime capture contracts

These contracts select the official published implementation, its normal headless entry, and a fresh configuration directory. They are source-verified capture recipes; Linux trace artifacts must establish success. A fake key is supplied only to make the real client assemble its request behind claude-tap. No real CLI or model invocation was performed locally. Preserve model/provider defaults when a single credential selects a supported native route. Record explicitly configured routes, local backends, and tool-enabling modes as named surfaces.

### Cline 3.0.74

Install npm `cline@3.0.74`, whose executable is `cline` and Linux binary dependency is `@cline/cli-linux-x64@3.0.74`. The matching official source tag is `cli-v3.0.74`. Do not substitute `@cline/cli@0.0.13`: that older package exposes `clite`, a different entry. [Published package](https://registry.npmjs.org/cline/3.0.74), [release CLI source](https://github.com/cline/cline/blob/cli-v3.0.74/apps/cli/src/main.ts).

```text
cline --json "Reply with one short sentence."
```

Add `--data-dir <fresh-absolute-directory>` to both setup and capture. This release-supported isolation option redirects database/session/team state and sets `CLINE_PROVIDER_SETTINGS_PATH` to that directory's `settings/providers.json`; setting only `CLINE_DATA_DIR` does not activate the helper. The CLI resolves the last-used configured provider, otherwise the Cline gateway. Headless single-task execution supplies the normal agent/tool registration and defaults to its noninteractive permission mode. Attempt the fresh Cline gateway by also adding `--key phistory-fake-api-key`, leaving provider/model unset; gateway catalog/account bootstrap may prevent a fake-credential run. This flag is an explicit release-supported auth path and does not depend on unverified gateway environment-key discovery. Its model comes from the provider's catalog/free/recommended selection, so do not call a hardcoded Anthropic model the product default. [Provider selection and key flag](https://github.com/cline/cline/blob/cli-v3.0.74/apps/cli/src/main.ts), [isolated settings paths](https://github.com/cline/cline/blob/cli-v3.0.74/apps/cli/src/utils/helpers.ts).

For an honest configured direct-Anthropic surface, the release's normal setup command is `cline auth --provider anthropic --apikey phistory-fake-api-key --modelid claude-sonnet-5-5`. That quick-auth branch saves the provider settings without validating a remote account. Run setup in Linux with the same isolated directory, then use the headless command without model/provider flags. An equivalent `settings/providers.json` is:

```json
{"version":1,"lastUsedProvider":"anthropic","providers":{"anthropic":{"settings":{"provider":"anthropic","apiKey":"phistory-fake-api-key","model":"claude-sonnet-5-5"},"updatedAt":"2026-11-17T00:00:00.000Z"}}}
```

The configured model is a recorded choice, not an unconfigured default. [Quick-auth implementation](https://github.com/cline/cline/blob/cli-v3.0.74/apps/cli/src/commands/auth.ts).

### Kilo Code 7.8.27

Install npm `@kilocode/cli@7.8.27`, executables `kilo` and `kilocode`, matching official tag `v7.8.27` / commit `03a6f07d053cd7e6aa88fa1e258e93510335259f`. Its headless run path builds the real session agent with registered tools. Model selection follows configured model, recent state, then available providers/models; it has no single fixed default across fresh authentication choices. [Package](https://registry.npmjs.org/@kilocode%2fcli/7.8.27), [run entry](https://github.com/Kilo-Org/kilocode/blob/v7.8.27/packages/opencode/src/cli/cmd/run.ts), [provider selection](https://github.com/Kilo-Org/kilocode/blob/v7.8.27/packages/opencode/src/provider/provider.ts).

```text
kilo run --format json "Reply with one short sentence."
```

Use isolated HOME/XDG directories. For a reproducible named OpenAI-compatible surface set `KILO_CONFIG_CONTENT` to the JSON below, `KILO_DISABLE_MODELS_FETCH=1`, and `KILO_DISABLE_AUTOUPDATE=1`. The compatible provider is bundled; it does not require an extra dynamically installed package. These configuration settings deliberately select a model and therefore must not be labeled Kilo's universal default.

```json
{"model":"phistory/gpt-4.1","enabled_providers":["phistory"],"provider":{"phistory":{"npm":"@ai-sdk/openai-compatible","name":"Phistory","options":{"baseURL":"https://api.openai.com/v1","apiKey":"phistory-fake-api-key"},"models":{"gpt-4.1":{"name":"GPT-4.1"}}}}}
```

Leave normal plugins enabled initially. `KILO_DISABLE_DEFAULT_PLUGINS=1` is a separately recorded fallback if plugin bootstrap blocks capture because disabling plugins may change the available tool surface. The initial request does not require an extra auto-approval flag. [Config loader](https://github.com/Kilo-Org/kilocode/blob/v7.8.27/packages/opencode/src/config/config.ts), [environment flags](https://github.com/Kilo-Org/kilocode/blob/v7.8.27/packages/core/src/flag/flag.ts).

### Letta Code 0.34.63, local backend

Install npm `@letta-ai/letta-code@0.34.63`, executable `letta`; its published `gitHead` is `eadef1c6c3594d1bf81517efd4b68fce54644ad4`. Cloud remains the documented default and requires Letta account/service state to create and manage its remote agent. The normal local backend is a distinct, directly capturable surface with its own MemFS agent state and prompt construction. [Package](https://registry.npmjs.org/@letta-ai%2fletta-code/0.34.63), [backend selection](https://github.com/letta-ai/letta-code/blob/eadef1c6c3594d1bf81517efd4b68fce54644ad4/src/index.ts).

```text
letta --backend local -p "Reply with one short sentence."
```

Set isolated HOME, `LETTA_LOCAL_BACKEND_DIR` to a fresh absolute state directory, and only `ANTHROPIC_API_KEY=phistory-fake-api-key`; omit `LETTA_API_KEY` and model flags. Local provider resolution chooses configured providers first. In this exact release the Anthropic default is `claude-opus-5-5`; the native OpenAI alternative is `gpt-6.1-sol`. The local backend invokes pi-ai directly and does not require a local Letta server or Cloud account. Preserve its normal MemFS memory tools; do not use the internal stateless-subagent memory kill switch to obtain an easier but narrower prompt. [Local provider/model resolution](https://github.com/letta-ai/letta-code/blob/eadef1c6c3594d1bf81517efd4b68fce54644ad4/src/backend/local/local-model-config.ts), [provider defaults](https://github.com/letta-ai/letta-code/blob/eadef1c6c3594d1bf81517efd4b68fce54644ad4/src/backend/dev/pi-provider-registry.ts), [state directory override](https://github.com/letta-ai/letta-code/blob/eadef1c6c3594d1bf81517efd4b68fce54644ad4/src/utils/local-backend-paths.ts).

### pi-rust 0.22.1

Install npm `@bigfishnpm/rpi@0.22.1`, executable `rpi`. Its installer downloads and checksums the matching official release binary; this captures the Rust agent loop rather than the separate existing Pi integration. [Package](https://registry.npmjs.org/@bigfishnpm%2frpi/0.22.1), [official release](https://github.com/bigfish1913/pi-rust/releases/tag/v0.22.1).

```text
rpi -p "Reply with one short sentence." --no-session
```

Set `RPI_CODING_AGENT_DIR` to a fresh absolute directory, `RPI_OFFLINE=1`, and only `ANTHROPIC_API_KEY=phistory-fake-api-key`. Offline mode disables startup network operations, not the model call. With no saved settings or model/provider flag, this credential selects the native Anthropic default `claude-opus-4-8`; the release also defines OpenAI's per-provider default `gpt-5.5`. Saved provider/model settings take precedence in a nonfresh home, which is why isolation matters. The print path preserves normal prompt/tool assembly. [Headless/offline arguments](https://github.com/bigfish1913/pi-rust/blob/v0.22.1/crates/rpi-cli/src/args.rs), [config paths](https://github.com/bigfish1913/pi-rust/blob/v0.22.1/crates/rpi-cli/src/config.rs), [runtime provider defaults and selection](https://github.com/bigfish1913/pi-rust/blob/v0.22.1/crates/rpi-runtime/src/provider.rs).

### Codewhale 0.10.2

Install npm `codewhale@0.10.2`, executables `codewhale` / `codew`, matching official tag `v0.10.2` and package `gitHead` `ff466b2ed0bfb4ae5267d4d8d0ecb562cf46956e`. The wrapper installs its own official Rust release. Native default provider is DeepSeek and the documented default model is `deepseek-flash`. [Package](https://registry.npmjs.org/codewhale/0.10.2), [release installation and headless reference](https://github.com/codewhale-hq/Codewhale/blob/v0.10.2/docs/INSTALL.md).

```text
codewhale exec --output-format stream-json "Reply with one short sentence."
codewhale exec --auto --output-format stream-json "Reply with one short sentence."
```

Record these as two surfaces: plain `exec` explicitly supplies no tools; `--auto` enables the agent's tool registry and its automatic permission mode. A successful tool-less default request therefore does not satisfy the tool-schema capture goal. Set `CODEWHALE_HOME` to an isolated directory and `DEEPSEEK_API_KEY=phistory-fake-api-key`. Its `config.toml` may contain `[update]` followed by `check_for_updates=false` to avoid updater bootstrap. Do not run `auth set` merely to persist the fake key: the release documentation describes a model-changing behavior in an older auth setup branch. Environment auth suffices in fresh state; existing explicit/config/secret-store keys outrank it. [Headless tool-mode distinction and credential priority](https://github.com/codewhale-hq/Codewhale/blob/v0.10.2/docs/INSTALL.md), [home isolation](https://github.com/codewhale-hq/Codewhale/blob/v0.10.2/crates/tui/src/config/home.rs).

### Prime Agent 0.10.3

Preserve the whole tree and symlink the command to the root executable; `PI_PACKAGE_DIR` may explicitly point to that extracted root.
```text
prime-agent --prime-agent-bootstrap

```

Run the bootstrap only in Linux setup, with `uv` available and network allowed, before the intercepted model request. It provisions the real Python kernel environment; the release includes its runtime sidecar but neither uv nor the ready venv. The exact bootstrap-only argument branches directly to `ensure_kernel_python` before normal auth/model construction and performs no model API call. Use the same isolated HOME and `PRIME_AGENT_CODING_AGENT_DIR` for setup/capture. Also isolate `PRIME_AGENT_SESSION_DIR` and `PRIME_AGENT_DAEMON_SOCKET`; the print path itself sets `daemonless=true` and prewarms its kernel. `PRIME_AGENT_KERNEL_VENV` can select a dedicated writable venv directory. [Official installer prewarm](https://github.com/PrimeIntellect-ai/prime-agent/blob/v0.10.3/install-rust.sh), [bootstrap entry](https://github.com/PrimeIntellect-ai/prime-agent/blob/v0.10.3/crates/pa-cli/src/lib.rs), [kernel provisioning](https://github.com/PrimeIntellect-ai/prime-agent/blob/v0.10.3/crates/pa-core/src/kernel/bootstrap/mod.rs).

For capture set `PI_OFFLINE=1` and only `ANTHROPIC_API_KEY=phistory-fake-api-key`; leave model/provider flags unset. The preferred global default is Prime Inference `z-ai/glm-5.3` when authorized, but available models are filtered by configured auth. With only Anthropic auth, its native per-provider default is `claude-opus-4-7`. The real kernel-backed tool surface includes `python_repl`; no faux-script seam is appropriate for capture. [Default model resolution](https://github.com/PrimeIntellect-ai/prime-agent/blob/v0.10.3/crates/pa-core/src/models/resolver.rs), [authenticated model filtering](https://github.com/PrimeIntellect-ai/prime-agent/blob/v0.10.3/crates/pa-core/src/models/registry.rs), [state/environment contract](https://github.com/PrimeIntellect-ai/prime-agent/blob/v0.10.3/crates/pa-cli/src/config.rs).

### OpenInterpreter Rust fork 0.0.61

Use official `rust-v0.0.61` / commit `80d8f55385452457a625dbabc032f3003b9545b4`, production Linux asset `open-interpreter-package-x86_64-unknown-linux-musl.tar.gz`. Preserve `codex-package.json`, `bin/interpreter`, `bin/codex-code-mode-host`, and `codex-resources` in the packaged layout. The old Python OpenInterpreter package and upstream `@openai/codex` package are different products; neither substitutes for this survey target. [Release](https://github.com/openinterpreter/openinterpreter/releases/tag/rust-v0.0.61), [official installation entry](https://www.openinterpreter.com/install), [Rust CLI](https://github.com/openinterpreter/openinterpreter/blob/rust-v0.0.61/codex-rs/cli/src/main.rs).

```text
interpreter exec --skip-git-repo-check --ephemeral --json "Reply with one short sentence."
```

Set `INTERPRETER_HOME` to a precreated isolated absolute directory, `OPENAI_API_KEY=phistory-fake-api-key`, and `OPEN_INTERPRETER_NONINTERACTIVE=1`. Interpreter deliberately ignores `CODEX_HOME` and otherwise uses `~/.openinterpreter`. Leave model/provider flags unset for the native catalog-selected default. [Exec arguments](https://github.com/openinterpreter/openinterpreter/blob/rust-v0.0.61/codex-rs/exec/src/cli.rs), [Interpreter home selection](https://github.com/openinterpreter/openinterpreter/blob/rust-v0.0.61/codex-rs/utils/home-dir/src/lib.rs).

The native OpenAI route uses Responses over WebSocket: `responses_websocket_enabled()` checks provider capability and session fallback state, with no feature or auth-mode gate. The legacy `responses_websockets` and `responses_websockets_v2` features are removed, so setting them false does not force HTTP. Linux validation of the actual `rust-v0.0.61` release binary rejected an attempted `[model_providers.openai]` override because the built-in ID is reserved; that earlier proposed override is invalid and must be removed. The tagged provider merge helper appears to permit replacement, so this is a release-binary/source discrepancy still requiring attribution; the observed binary takes precedence. [Transport gate](https://github.com/openinterpreter/openinterpreter/blob/rust-v0.0.61/codex-rs/core/src/client.rs), [removed legacy features](https://github.com/openinterpreter/openinterpreter/blob/rust-v0.0.61/codex-rs/features/src/lib.rs), [tagged merge helper](https://github.com/openinterpreter/openinterpreter/blob/rust-v0.0.61/codex-rs/model-provider-info/src/lib.rs).

Prefer a capture-only WebSocket guard that refuses the upgrade with HTTP **426 before opening any upstream connection**, followed by the CLI's native HTTP fallback. Tagged `core/src/client.rs` explicitly maps `TransportError::Http` with `StatusCode::UPGRADE_REQUIRED` to `WebsocketStreamOutcome::FallbackToHttp`; both `prewarm_websocket()` and the normal `stream()` switch the session fallback state, and `stream()` then calls `stream_responses_api()` with the same prompt, model and provider. The WebSocket connector maps unsuccessful HTTP handshakes into that transport error. This preserves the native catalog selection and headers without rewriting provider configuration; Linux capture must verify the emitted HTTP request. The existing tap WebSocket path must not be used until its capture-only guard prevents upstream forwarding. [426 and HTTP fallback branches](https://github.com/openinterpreter/openinterpreter/blob/rust-v0.0.61/codex-rs/core/src/client.rs#L1916), [connector](https://github.com/openinterpreter/openinterpreter/blob/rust-v0.0.61/codex-rs/codex-api/src/endpoint/responses_websocket.rs).

If a separately named custom-provider fallback is needed, retain Responses, OpenAI auth, native standalone search and headers, explicitly select that custom provider, and label the result accordingly. `0.159.0` is this tag's `OPEN_INTERPRETER_CODEX_COMPATIBILITY_VERSION`, not its product version: derive it from the exact release source and retain its hash rather than hardcoding it across future releases. `codex-package.json` records product version and layout paths, with no compatibility-version field. A native 426 fallback avoids that configuration duplication. [Compatibility constant](https://github.com/openinterpreter/openinterpreter/blob/rust-v0.0.61/codex-rs/product-info/src/lib.rs), [package metadata schema](https://github.com/openinterpreter/openinterpreter/blob/rust-v0.0.61/scripts/codex_package/layout.py).

## Linux validation repairs: pi-rust and OpenDev

The Linux follow-up reached pi-rust's HTTPS transport failure before any tap model request and OpenDev's first-run wizard despite the fake provider credential. These require transport/config setup corrections, not static extraction or upstream runtime patches.

For pi-rust `0.22.1`, keep the native Anthropic provider/model and set `ANTHROPIC_BASE_URL=http://api.anthropic.com` **only under the capture-only forward proxy**. The built-in Anthropic override changes the endpoint for `AnthropicMessages` models without changing provider identity or prompt selection. Its provider builds `<base>/v1/messages`, so the override must not include `/v1`. Runtime proxy resolution explicitly honors the HTTP target's `http_proxy`/`HTTP_PROXY`; this removes the HTTPS MITM certificate requirement while retaining the native Messages body. The release uses reqwest `0.12` with `default-features=false` and `rustls-tls`, and its client builder does not load `SSL_CERT_FILE` or a custom CA environment variable. No unverified CA flag is proposed. Record the HTTP capture transport in provenance. [Endpoint and proxy construction](https://github.com/bigfish1913/pi-rust/blob/v0.22.1/crates/rpi-runtime/src/provider.rs), [Anthropic URL construction](https://github.com/bigfish1913/pi-rust/blob/v0.22.1/crates/rpi-ai/src/providers/anthropic/mod.rs), [TLS dependency](https://github.com/bigfish1913/pi-rust/blob/v0.22.1/Cargo.toml).

For OpenDev `0.1.9`, set `OPENDEV_DIR=<isolated-absolute-state>` and create `<OPENDEV_DIR>/settings.json` containing exactly `{}` before `opendev -p ...`. The noninteractive runner invokes setup whenever `setup::config_exists()` is false, independently of `FIREWORKS_API_KEY`. That existence check uses `Paths::default().global_settings().exists()`; `OPENDEV_DIR` pins config/data/cache/state roots, and `global_settings` is `<root>/settings.json`. The loader starts from `AppConfig::default()` and merges partial JSON, so `{}` retains native Fireworks/Kimi model, tools and other defaults. Keep fake `FIREWORKS_API_KEY` and `OPENDEV_DISABLE_REMOTE_MODELS=1`; no model/provider override is needed. [Noninteractive first-run gate](https://github.com/opendev-to/opendev/blob/v0.1.9/crates/opendev-cli/src/runners.rs), [existence check](https://github.com/opendev-to/opendev/blob/v0.1.9/crates/opendev-cli/src/setup/mod.rs), [path override](https://github.com/opendev-to/opendev/blob/v0.1.9/crates/opendev-config/src/paths.rs), [default-preserving JSON merge](https://github.com/opendev-to/opendev/blob/v0.1.9/crates/opendev-config/src/loader/mod.rs).

## Third runtime batch: source-owned task entry points

Follow-up [44086915151](https://github.com/DragonnZhang/phistory/actions/runs/44086915151) captured pi-rust, OpenDev and Letta local (26 survey integrations), plus Codewhale auto and Cline Anthropic. Fresh smoke [44087559360](https://github.com/DragonnZhang/phistory/actions/runs/44087559360) passed all five agents and all seven selected variants. pi-rust's six tools, Codewhale's 0/15 tools, Cline's 26/26 tools and Letta's 22 tools match exactly. OpenDev's seven tool definitions match by name; only the upstream hash-map iteration order differs. Preserve the raw order.

Run [44087561017](https://github.com/DragonnZhang/phistory/actions/runs/44087561017) added Fibona minimal/terminal, CTXAgent paper API, Quorum standard discussion, Tianshu minimal/full and Headlong's native monolith wake, reaching 31 survey integrations. Their tool counts are 1/1, 47, 0, 26/43 and 0. All nine Quorum phase requests and all Fibona bootstrap/repair requests remain in the raw traces. Headlong's fenced-Bash action protocol is embedded in its captured system instructions. Reasonix failed its stored-key preflight, Octomind needed its official tap dependencies before interception, and OpenAgent's local sign-in encoding failed; these remain pending fixes rather than successful captures.

Second-batch status: [44085421301](https://github.com/DragonnZhang/phistory/actions/runs/44085421301) archived Jcode, Codewhale default, Kilo, Cline's native gateway and Prime Agent, bringing the survey to 23 runtime integrations. Independent fresh smoke [44086200013](https://github.com/DragonnZhang/phistory/actions/runs/44086200013) succeeded for all five; canonical tools match exactly (27, 0, 23, 26 and 1 respectively). Prime's normalized prompt matches exactly; other differences are current time/CPU or isolated temporary workspace names. Codewhale's zero tools is its real plain-exec behavior; its auto variant still needs capture. pi-rust failed TLS before a request and OpenDev entered onboarding; the source-backed setup corrections are recorded below. Empty Actions input selected `default` rather than agent policy, so Letta local/Interpreter HTTP were not attempted in that run. The workflow now uses explicit `auto` policy selection.

All eight have concrete native task/wake entry points. Four use terminal tasks directly; Headlong exposes a synchronous framework wake; CTXAgent and Quorum expose Python runtime APIs; OpenAgent needs its local server and Store API. These are source-verified recipes pending Linux validation. They construct the real runtime and let it emit the request; none substitutes static prompt concatenation for a trace.

### Fibona: Python bootstrap runtime and separate JavaScript port

The current official release is `js-v0.1.3`, with `fibona-agent-0.1.3.tgz` for the JavaScript library. Its root Python package remains version `0.1.0`, Python ≥3.12, with console script `fibona = fibona:main`; the survey's Python runtime is source-installable from this tag. Install the source/lock in Linux, not an unrelated npm package: the npm registry name returned no published package, while the official JS README installs the release tgz directly. [Release](https://github.com/FibonaAI/fibona-agent/releases/tag/js-v0.1.3), [Python packaging](https://github.com/FibonaAI/fibona-agent/blob/js-v0.1.3/pyproject.toml), [JS packaging and installation](https://github.com/FibonaAI/fibona-agent/blob/js-v0.1.3/js/README.md).

```text
fibona --boot-mode minimal "Reply with one short sentence."
```

Set `FIBONA_API_KEY=phistory-fake-api-key`, isolated HOME/cwd, and `FIBONA_LOG_DIR`. Leave `FIBONA_MODEL` and `FIBONA_BASE_URL` unset: native defaults are `gpt-6-astra` and OpenAI `/v1`. `--boot-mode` is required; `minimal` and `terminal` are explicit native bootstrap surfaces, not an implicit default. The real CLI creates `Mind`, `IPythonEnv`, and `Agent.run`; minimal reads the positional task then calls `call_me`. Its emitted request is nonstreaming Responses, `store=false`, complete native instructions, and the `set_next_cell` tool schema. Terminal mode additionally contributes its normal bootstrap instructions. [CLI construction](https://github.com/FibonaAI/fibona-agent/blob/js-v0.1.3/fibona.py), [minimal bootstrap](https://github.com/FibonaAI/fibona-agent/blob/js-v0.1.3/bootstraps/minimal/init.py), [native mind/tool request](https://github.com/FibonaAI/fibona-agent/blob/js-v0.1.3/core/agent.py).

A text-only dummy response does not choose a next cell, so the native runtime repairs until its default five consecutive failures and can exit nonzero after valid captures. A focused API driver can instead use the public `Agent.run(..., on_result=...)` observer to stop after the first bootstrap result, retaining normal bootstrap/Mind execution. Do not use `Mind.query`/`call_llm_api`: that intentionally omits agent instructions and the host tool. The JS port's documented `Agent(...).run(BOOTSTRAP, {env})` is a separate feasible library surface.

### Octomind 0.72.0

Use release asset `octomind-0.72.0-x86_64-unknown-linux-musl.tar.gz`. Native configuration now defaults to model `octohub:auto` and role `assistant:chief`; `developer` resolves a different domain orchestrator and `developer:general` is a named specialist. Scripts skip interactive onboarding. [Release](https://github.com/Muvon/octomind/releases/tag/0.72.0), [native default configuration](https://github.com/Muvon/octomind/blob/0.72.0/config-templates/default.toml), [run arguments](https://github.com/Muvon/octomind/blob/0.72.0/src/commands/run.rs).

```text
printf '%s\n' 'Reply with one short sentence.' | octomind run --format jsonl -c model.request_transport=http
```

Set isolated `OCTOMIND_DATA_DIR`, `OCTOHUB_API_KEY=phistory-fake-api-key`, and `OCTOMIND_TELEMETRY=0`. There is no positional task argument: the sole positional argument is a role/tag, and headless input comes from nonempty stdin. The explicit `http` transport is necessary for capture safety because the native model profile defaults to WebSocket; keep model/provider/role selections native. Dotted `-c` overrides accept unquoted strings when TOML parsing fails. The locked octolib `0.43.1` OctoHub HTTP client sends Responses-style `instructions`, `input`, and full tools to `/v1/completions`; claude-tap's existing OpenAI path and Responses dummy shape fit this route. [Override parsing](https://github.com/Muvon/octomind/blob/0.72.0/src/config/loading.rs), [state/auth environment](https://github.com/Muvon/octomind/blob/0.72.0/doc/reference/04-environment-variables.md), [OctoHub HTTP request](https://github.com/Muvon/octolib/blob/0.43.1/src/llm/providers/octohub.rs).

Before interception, clone the official default tap into `<OCTOMIND_DATA_DIR>/taps/muvon/octomind-tap` using the same shallow Git clone the source uses. Record its commit: tap manifests independently determine prompt, tools, and dependencies. This public-source bootstrap performs no model call. Do not call `octomind tap muvon/tap`; source explicitly rejects re-adding its always-active built-in tap. Subsequent dependency/capability startup still needs Linux verification with the selected tap manifest. [Default tap cloning and location](https://github.com/Muvon/octomind/blob/0.72.0/src/agent/taps.rs), [official tap](https://github.com/muvon/octomind-tap).

Linux startup exposed a required second bootstrap: run `bash <tap>/deps/muvon/octobrain.sh` **before interception**, with the same HOME and `$HOME/.local/bin` on PATH. At inspected tap commit `6950a8f44594cfb8709cf327f9a45cfe94836ce4`, the default `assistant:chief` uses core/orchestration builtins and memory-read/write capabilities; their only external command dependency is `muvon/octobrain`. The official script exits immediately when `octobrain` is on PATH; otherwise it installs the latest `Muvon/octobrain` release asset `octobrain-${VERSION}-x86_64-unknown-linux-musl.tar.gz`, main binary `octobrain`. Record both dependency release and tap commit. MCP startup is `octobrain mcp`; its native manager is lazy, so tool-list construction needs no cloud model key. Optional background knowledge initialization uses default local FastEmbed weights and swallows initialization errors without blocking the session; it must not be silently replaced by a remote embedding model. [Default role](https://github.com/muvon/octomind-tap/blob/6950a8f44594cfb8709cf327f9a45cfe94836ce4/agents/assistant/chief.toml), [memory dependency](https://github.com/muvon/octomind-tap/blob/6950a8f44594cfb8709cf327f9a45cfe94836ce4/capabilities/memory-read/octobrain.toml), [official installer](https://github.com/muvon/octomind-tap/blob/6950a8f44594cfb8709cf327f9a45cfe94836ce4/deps/muvon/octobrain.sh), [Octobrain lazy MCP startup](https://github.com/Muvon/octobrain/blob/bfb15d6772028f00207b757e883e2d1ddd3fb562/src/commands.rs), [background initialization](https://github.com/Muvon/octobrain/blob/bfb15d6772028f00207b757e883e2d1ddd3fb562/src/mcp/server.rs).

### OpenAgent 2.89.2: local Store API

Use official binary `openagent_linux_x86_64` from `v2.89.2`. `openagent run`, `start`, `serve`, and `gateway` are aliases for its HTTP server; none is a one-task prompt command. No hosted account is needed: with no Casdoor endpoint, a fresh instance creates the native local `admin` account with initial password `123`. Fresh initialization creates a default Store and tools, but **no model provider**, so a configured model lane is required. [Release](https://github.com/the-open-agent/openagent/releases/tag/v2.89.2), [command dispatch](https://github.com/the-open-agent/openagent/blob/v2.89.2/internal/cli/cli.go), [local account initialization](https://github.com/the-open-agent/openagent/blob/v2.89.2/object/user.go), [Store/provider initialization](https://github.com/the-open-agent/openagent/blob/v2.89.2/object/init.go).

Start the server under tap, in isolated HOME/cwd, with exact configuration environment names `driverName=sqlite3` and `dataSourceName=<isolated-absolute-path>/openagent.db`. This explicit database path matters: the single-binary SQLite fallback otherwise writes beside the installed executable, outside HOME. Reserve its native port 14000 exclusively for the driver, or supply an isolated normal `conf/app.conf` with a chosen port. [Config environment precedence](https://github.com/the-open-agent/openagent/blob/v2.89.2/conf/conf.go), [database isolation](https://github.com/the-open-agent/openagent/blob/v2.89.2/object/adapter.go), [server startup](https://github.com/the-open-agent/openagent/blob/v2.89.2/main.go).

Use a focused local-HTTP driver with this sequence:

1. `POST /api/signin` with `{"username":"admin","password":"123"}` and retain its local session cookie.
2. `POST /api/add-provider` with `{"name":"phistory","category":"Model","type":"OpenAI","subType":"gpt-4.1","clientSecret":"phistory-fake-api-key","providerUrl":"https://api.openai.com","state":"Active","isDefault":true}`. This endpoint inserts the record; connection testing is a different endpoint.
3. `GET /api/get-store?id=admin/store-built-in`; preserve the returned native Store fields and tool selection. Set only `modelProvider="phistory"` and `externalApiKey="phistory-local-store-key"`, then `POST /api/update-store?id=admin/store-built-in` with that Store.
4. `POST /api/v1/chat/completions` with bearer `phistory-local-store-key` and body `{"model":"gpt-4.1","stream":false,"messages":[{"role":"user","content":"Reply with one short sentence."}]}`.

The Store-key route performs `MergeMcpTools` and `QueryTextWithTools`, preserving the actual Store prompt and available tool registration. The direct provider-key route calls `QueryText` without this harness/tool assembly and is unsuitable as a replacement. Record this as a configured local Store/API surface; no universal unconfigured model exists. [Password sign-in](https://github.com/the-open-agent/openagent/blob/v2.89.2/controllers/signin.go), [provider insertion](https://github.com/the-open-agent/openagent/blob/v2.89.2/controllers/provider.go), [Store read/update](https://github.com/the-open-agent/openagent/blob/v2.89.2/controllers/store.go), [Store versus provider completion paths](https://github.com/the-open-agent/openagent/blob/v2.89.2/controllers/openai_api.go).

The latest release advanced to `v2.95.1` during Linux validation. Its frontend still sends **plain JSON password `123`**, and the backend directly verifies bcrypt; there is no password base64/encryption step. The observed `/api/signin` response `illegal base64 data at input byte 0` remains a separate release/runtime failure requiring retained logs. A source-supported alternate setup surface configures `clientId=phistory-local-client`, `clientSecret=phistory-local-client-secret`, `casdoorApplication=phistory`, and no issuer/Casdoor endpoint. Authenticate the three setup endpoints with normal HTTP Basic `base64(clientId:clientSecret)` instead of the sign-in sequence. Native `AutoSigninFilter` compares those configured application credentials locally and creates admin application claims; it does not call the hosted identity service. Use only the Store bearer on the final completion request, which this filter explicitly skips. Name this the **configured local application-auth Store surface**, preserving the same native Store prompt/tool route. [Unencoded frontend password](https://github.com/the-open-agent/openagent/blob/v2.95.1/web/src/backend/AccountBackend.js), [local credential comparison and claims](https://github.com/the-open-agent/openagent/blob/v2.95.1/routers/base.go), [native authentication filter](https://github.com/the-open-agent/openagent/blob/v2.95.1/routers/auto_signin_filter.go), [admin role check](https://github.com/the-open-agent/openagent/blob/v2.95.1/util/permission.go).

That application-auth path passed Linux authentication, provider insertion and Store reading. The subsequent SQLite `UpdateStore` failed with `Not supported multiple primary keys`: its implementation uses `ID(core.PK{owner, name}).AllCols().Update(...)`. Use the normal **AddStore clone** path instead: copy the returned built-in Store, retain all prompt/tool fields, and set `owner="admin"`, `name="phistory-capture"`, `isDefault=false`, `modelProvider="phistory"`, and the fake `externalApiKey`. Before insertion, omit `fileTree` and `propertiesMap`: the official `ForkStore` path explicitly resets both to `nil` on its copied Store, whereas `Populate()` constructs a nonempty `FileTree` for a GET response. A literal GET-to-POST clone failed Linux insertion with `no primary key for col file_tree`; these native clone resets address that separate failure without changing prompt/tools. `POST /api/add-store` performs normal provider synchronization and `engine.Insert(store)`; the failing composite-key update is absent. Read back `admin/phistory-capture` to verify prompt/tool configuration and use its Store bearer for the task. Record the named clone, not an update of the built-in row. [Native AddStore controller](https://github.com/the-open-agent/openagent/blob/v2.95.1/controllers/store.go#L393), [insert versus update implementations](https://github.com/the-open-agent/openagent/blob/v2.95.1/object/store.go#L341), [native clone field resets](https://github.com/the-open-agent/openagent/blob/v2.95.1/object/store_fork.go#L159), [file-tree population](https://github.com/the-open-agent/openagent/blob/v2.95.1/object/store_provider.go#L92).

### Headlong: synchronous native monolith wake

No GitHub release is published; pin current official source commit `72f599562d104c7785798dde63dd5c1c7562280a`. Put its `bin/` and `tools/` on PATH with Bash, curl, jq, Python 3, and Git available. The ordinary checkout installer without `--init` is another supported way to install tools. Avoid running the identity interview/bootstrap installer outside tap because it can invoke models. [Checkout installation](https://github.com/laude-institute/headlong/blob/72f599562d104c7785798dde63dd5c1c7562280a/docs/install.md), [framework versus shellm naming](https://github.com/laude-institute/headlong/blob/72f599562d104c7785798dde63dd5c1c7562280a/AGENTS.md).

```text
identity --dir <state>/identities new phistory
bash -c '. "$1"; thinkers step monolith' _ <state>/identities/phistory/activate
```

The first command is Linux setup: it creates the native identity, root trajectory, bundled kernel/thinker templates, and literal seed thoughts without any model call. Run the second command under tap with isolated HOME/`HEADLONG_HOME`, only `ANTHROPIC_API_KEY=phistory-fake-api-key`, `SHELLM_THINKER_ENV=local`, and `SHELLM_MAX_ITERATIONS=1`. It executes a real manual-trigger monolith wake synchronously, with no dispatcher daemon. Native model selection remains `claude-opus-4-7`. The explicit local execution environment and iteration bound belong in provenance. [Identity creation and activation](https://github.com/laude-institute/headlong/blob/72f599562d104c7785798dde63dd5c1c7562280a/tools/identity), [synchronous step command](https://github.com/laude-institute/headlong/blob/72f599562d104c7785798dde63dd5c1c7562280a/bin/thinkers), [monolith prompt/wake assembly](https://github.com/laude-institute/headlong/blob/72f599562d104c7785798dde63dd5c1c7562280a/thinkers/monolith/step).

This captures the framework's actual persona/memory/recent-stream/function-menu context. The underlying action protocol is fenced Bash with `FINAL`/`FINAL_FILE`, not API JSON function schemas; zero API `tools` is genuine. A lower-level `shellm --env local --max-iterations 1 TEXT` is independently feasible but should remain a separate engine surface. Standalone shellm also makes an asynchronous run-summary request, whereas the monolith reuses its existing root trajectory; preserve auxiliary requests when present. [Native shellm prompt and loop](https://github.com/laude-institute/headlong/blob/72f599562d104c7785798dde63dd5c1c7562280a/bin/shellm), [protocol reference](https://github.com/laude-institute/headlong/blob/72f599562d104c7785798dde63dd5c1c7562280a/docs/shellm.md).

### CTXAgent: paper runtime Python task API

The survey references root `ctxagent/`, the frozen paper runtime, package version `0.1.0`; official release `app-v0.2.1` also contains a separately developed `app/ctxagent/` desktop runtime. There is no published PyPI package at `ctxagent`. Source-install the root editable package from this tag, with Python ≥3.11. The `ctxagent` console command opens its GUI/server; use its actual public `AgentSession.run_turn` API for a bounded task. [Root packaging](https://github.com/MatthiasHBusch/CTXAgent/blob/app-v0.2.1/pyproject.toml), [paper/application distinction](https://github.com/MatthiasHBusch/CTXAgent/blob/app-v0.2.1/app/README.md).

Set isolated `CTXAGENT_DATA`, HOME, and workdir, plus `OPENROUTER_API_KEY=phistory-fake-api-key`. In Linux setup, call `asyncio.run(ctxagent.providers.providers(force=True))` before interception to cache the real public `/api/v1/providers` directory; it performs no generation. The native `eu_us` policy refuses an empty provider allowlist, so replacing that GET with a generic dummy can otherwise prevent the model request. Preserve the normal policy; the native disk cache lasts 24 hours. [Provider-directory cache](https://github.com/MatthiasHBusch/CTXAgent/blob/app-v0.2.1/ctxagent/providers.py), [routing guard](https://github.com/MatthiasHBusch/CTXAgent/blob/app-v0.2.1/ctxagent/flex.py).

```python
import asyncio
from pathlib import Path
from ctxagent import store
from ctxagent.agent import AgentSession
from ctxagent.config import SessionConfig
from ctxagent.segments import Conversation

async def main():
    store.init()
    cfg = SessionConfig(workdirs=[str(Path.cwd())])
    cid = store.create_chat("Phistory", cfg.to_dict())
    session = AgentSession(cid, cfg, Conversation())
    async for event in session.run_turn("Reply with one short sentence."):
        print(event)

asyncio.run(main())
```

The real runtime constructs its system segment, compaction engine, workspace and tool schema, then streams Chat Completions through OpenRouter. The paper default is `anthropic/claude-sonnet-5`; do not substitute the newer desktop app's `anthropic/claude-opus-5.5` default. Tool availability follows the normally installed optional readers and workspace state; record those conditions. This is an honest paper/API capture and does not claim the newer desktop surface. [AgentSession runtime](https://github.com/MatthiasHBusch/CTXAgent/blob/app-v0.2.1/ctxagent/agent.py), [native defaults/state](https://github.com/MatthiasHBusch/CTXAgent/blob/app-v0.2.1/ctxagent/config.py), [native HTTP client](https://github.com/MatthiasHBusch/CTXAgent/blob/app-v0.2.1/ctxagent/llm.py).

### DeepSeek-Reasonix Studio 2.61.0

Use `studio-v2.61.0` asset `reasonix-linux-amd64.tar.gz`, the current 2.x CLI sharing the Studio engine. npm `reasonix` belongs to the maintained 1.x channel and must not replace the survey's Studio implementation. [Official release](https://github.com/esengine/DeepSeek-Reasonix/releases/tag/studio-v2.61.0), [channel distinction](https://github.com/esengine/DeepSeek-Reasonix/blob/studio-v2.61.0/README.md).

```text
reasonix -p "Reply with one short sentence." --output-format json
```

Set `REASONIX_HOME` to an isolated absolute directory and write `<REASONIX_HOME>/.env`, mode 0600, containing `DEEPSEEK_API_KEY=phistory-fake-api-key` and a final newline. An outer process environment key alone is insufficient: Linux validation failed before a request, and the matching source deliberately resolves provider credentials only from Reasonix's global credential file. `REASONIX_CREDENTIALS_STORE=file` makes this store selection explicit. Leave model/provider/preset flags unset. Native configuration defaults to `deepseek-flash` and built-in DeepSeek providers using the OpenAI-compatible route. [Stored-key resolution](https://github.com/esengine/DeepSeek-Reasonix/blob/studio-v2.61.0/internal/contract/config/credentials.go), [credential file path](https://github.com/esengine/DeepSeek-Reasonix/blob/studio-v2.61.0/internal/contract/config/paths.go), [native defaults and validation](https://github.com/esengine/DeepSeek-Reasonix/blob/studio-v2.61.0/internal/contract/config/config.go).

Setting `REASONIX_HOME` explicitly skips legacy migration and OS-home scanning and isolates all config/state/cache. The print/run path uses the normal engine; unlike Codewhale, it has no documented plain-exec no-tools restriction. `--max-steps 1` is an optional recorded native tool-round bound; external capture timeout can preserve the default budget. Missing language servers stay dormant rather than preventing the first request. [Print/run CLI](https://github.com/esengine/DeepSeek-Reasonix/blob/studio-v2.61.0/docs/CLI.md), [state isolation](https://github.com/esengine/DeepSeek-Reasonix/blob/studio-v2.61.0/docs/CONFIG_PATHS.md).

### Quorum CLI 1.5.1: native API-participant discussion

Install PyPI `quorum-cli==1.5.1`, Python ≥3.11. Its default `quorum` command launches a Node TUI; official alternatives are `quorum --ipc`, `quorum-mcp-server`, and the Python discussion runtime. Use `FourPhaseConsensusTeam.run_stream` directly for an ordinary configured debate without Node or a fabricated prompt. There is no universal fresh default participant lineup: models are required configured choices. [PyPI release](https://pypi.org/project/quorum-cli/1.5.1/), [entry points](https://github.com/Detrol/quorum-cli/blob/v1.5.1/pyproject.toml), [native discussion API](https://github.com/Detrol/quorum-cli/blob/v1.5.1/src/quorum/team.py).

Set isolated HOME and workdir, `OPENAI_API_KEY=phistory-fake-api-key`, and `OPENAI_MODELS=gpt-4.1,gpt-4.1-mini`. The fake credential satisfies its ≥20-character format check. A supported optional bound is `QUORUM_ROUNDS_PER_AGENT=1` (native default 2). A focused driver is:

```python
import asyncio
from quorum.team import FourPhaseConsensusTeam
from quorum.models import close_pool

async def main():
    team = FourPhaseConsensusTeam(
        model_ids=["openai:gpt-4.1", "openai:gpt-4.1-mini"],
        method_override="standard",
    )
    try:
        async for event in team.run_stream("Reply with one short sentence."):
            print(event)
    finally:
        await close_pool()

asyncio.run(main())
```

This runs the actual independent-answer, critique, discussion, final-position and synthesis phases using its pooled OpenAI client, with native nonstreaming Chat Completions. It does not need the UI's separate model-validation ping. API/local-model participants intentionally have no tool access, so empty API tools are correct; delegated agent-CLI participants and host-facing MCP tools are different surfaces. Preserve phase requests rather than keeping only the first or synthesis prompt. [Standard phase execution](https://github.com/Detrol/quorum-cli/blob/v1.5.1/src/quorum/methods/standard.py), [client/model selection](https://github.com/Detrol/quorum-cli/blob/v1.5.1/src/quorum/models.py), [native OpenAI requests](https://github.com/Detrol/quorum-cli/blob/v1.5.1/src/quorum/clients/openai_client.py), [config validation](https://github.com/Detrol/quorum-cli/blob/v1.5.1/src/quorum/config.py).
