Nausicaa places its main instructions in a developer message; MiniAGI places the action grammar in the user context; CUGA's codeact system text includes both official example function definitions. These are preserved in prompt Markdown and raw requests. All fully verified latest policies now track daily; Cheetah's newly added native Ollama default remains pending its own capture.

Verification milestone: [44088809994](https://github.com/DragonnZhang/phistory/actions/runs/44088809994) captured and independently smoke-tested Interpreter's native HTTP fallback (GPT-6.1 Sol, 13 tools) and Reasonix's native DeepSeek Flash route (19 tools), reaching 33 survey integrations. All canonical tool definitions match exactly. These verified latest policies now enter scheduled capture.

These eight have concrete native task paths. A configured model lane, example SDK toolset, or local-core variant must retain that name in provenance; it is not evidence for every deployment's defaults. The commands below are source-derived Linux capture contracts, pending emitted-request validation.

### Nausicaa Harness 0.2.0

Install official `nausicaa-harness@0.2.0` with Node `>=22.19.0`; the executable is `nausicaa`. Native one-shot entry is `nausicaa --print TEXT` (`--json` also supported). Set `NAUSICAA_MODEL=openai:gpt-5.4`, fake `OPENAI_API_KEY`, and isolated HOME/cwd. There is no universal default model: settings resolution requires a previously saved choice or `NAUSICAA_MODEL`; the selected OpenAI lane must be recorded. The normal setup alternative is `nausicaa config set-model openai:gpt-5.4`, which writes configuration without an inference task. [Official npm metadata](https://registry.npmjs.org/nausicaa-harness/0.2.0), [CLI](https://github.com/jackispm/nausicaa-harness/blob/v0.2.0/src/cli.ts), [settings resolution](https://github.com/jackispm/nausicaa-harness/blob/v0.2.0/src/config/settings.ts).

User state is under `~/.nausicaa`; project settings/data are under cwd's `.nausicaa`. Preserve default observer behavior: Teto is enabled and its model inherits the main model when unset, including in print mode. Keep every native observer/main request rather than extracting only a convenient prompt. The runtime delegates provider serialization to its pinned `@mariozechner/pi-ai`, not a hand-built prompt exporter. [User settings](https://github.com/jackispm/nausicaa-harness/blob/v0.2.0/src/config/user-settings.ts), [provider model construction](https://github.com/jackispm/nausicaa-harness/blob/v0.2.0/src/model/pi-ai-model.ts), [package dependencies](https://github.com/jackispm/nausicaa-harness/blob/v0.2.0/package.json).

### Cheetahclaws 3.5.90

Official PyPI package is `cheetahclaws==3.5.90`, Python `>=3.10`. Native headless command is `cheetahclaws -p TEXT --output-format json --max-turns 1`; `exec` is an alias. `-p` bypasses first-run TTY onboarding and invokes the actual agent/tool runtime. Its fresh configuration defaults to **`ollama/gemma4:e4b`**, not a cloud model. The native Ollama `/api/chat` NDJSON surface needs protocol/dummy/export support beyond the tap's present OpenAI/Anthropic/Gemini protocols. [Package](https://pypi.org/project/cheetahclaws/3.5.90/), [CLI](https://github.com/SAIL-Research-Lab/cheetahclaws/blob/v3.5.90/cheetahclaws/cli.py), [defaults](https://github.com/SAIL-Research-Lab/cheetahclaws/blob/v3.5.90/cheetahclaws/config.py).

An immediately practical, honestly named configured-Anthropic lane uses isolated HOME, `~/.cheetahclaws/config.json` containing `{"model":"claude-sonnet-4-6"}`, and fake `ANTHROPIC_API_KEY`. Model-prefix detection selects Anthropic Messages; provider code reads the normal key environment variable. Leave other configuration defaults in force. The native headless path hides tools requiring a person at the keyboard, so its emitted toolset is the actual headless set rather than the interactive keyboard set. [Headless runtime](https://github.com/SAIL-Research-Lab/cheetahclaws/blob/v3.5.90/cheetahclaws/headless.py), [provider dispatch and key handling](https://github.com/SAIL-Research-Lab/cheetahclaws/blob/v3.5.90/cheetahclaws/providers.py).

For native Ollama capture, supported endpoint precedence is `OLLAMA_BASE_URL`, saved `ollama_base_url`, then `http://localhost:11434`. With native `/api/chat` NDJSON tap support, a recorded capture-only override such as `OLLAMA_BASE_URL=http://ollama.phistory.invalid` lets the forward proxy intercept the HTTP request while preserving model `gemma4:e4b` and the native tool profile. This is a reserved dummy transport host, not a model service. Headless `-p` performs no `/api/tags` preflight: the model picker is interactive-only, and `stream_ollama()` directly constructs system/messages, tools, `stream:true` and `options.num_ctx`, then POSTs `/api/chat` with no auth. The response parser reads line-delimited JSON `message.content`, `message.thinking` and native tool calls; it does not expect SSE. [Ollama request and endpoint dispatch](https://github.com/SAIL-Research-Lab/cheetahclaws/blob/v3.5.90/cheetahclaws/providers.py#L1922), [headless dispatch](https://github.com/SAIL-Research-Lab/cheetahclaws/blob/v3.5.90/cheetahclaws/cli.py#L2463).

### Trashclaw source CLI

Use the official single-file source at `6dce9edbd7c6633da75971156911636a7b073792`; no package installation or third-party dependencies are needed. Run `python trashclaw.py --exec TEXT` with isolated HOME/cwd and optionally `TRASHCLAW_MAX_ROUNDS=1`. `--exec` enters `agent_turn()` and the native prompt/tool loop, returning before interactive backend discovery and plugin loading. Built-in function schemas are still passed by `_available_tools()`. For the capture-only forward tap, record `TRASHCLAW_URL=https://api.openai.com/v1` as an explicit transport override, allowing its actual streaming `/v1/chat/completions` request to be intercepted without a local model server. The source sends **no `model` field and no Authorization header**: its displayed model name `local` is not a selected cloud model. Do not invent an API key or classify this capture as the OpenAI product's default harness. A reverse-tap local endpoint is another valid transport option when supported. [Native payload, URL and streaming parser](https://github.com/Scottcjn/trashclaw/blob/6dce9edbd7c6633da75971156911636a7b073792/trashclaw.py#L2065).

### MiniAGI public `think()` API

Install the official source at `f012aa41e46a7b7dea99d1358305f2fb605a6599` using its `requirements.txt`; Python 3.11 is the practical choice for its old scientific wheels. The source pins ThinkGPT 0.0.6, OpenAI 0.27.6 and tiktoken 0.3.3; ThinkGPT itself requires LangChain 0.0.141. Preserve those dependencies instead of substituting modern SDK APIs. Set a fake `OPENAI_API_KEY`, isolate HOME/cwd, and pre-cache `tiktoken.encoding_for_model('gpt-4')` during setup so a public tokenizer-asset download cannot be mistaken for a model call. [Source](https://github.com/muellerberndt/mini-agi/tree/f012aa41e46a7b7dea99d1358305f2fb605a6599), [requirements](https://github.com/muellerberndt/mini-agi/blob/f012aa41e46a7b7dea99d1358305f2fb605a6599/requirements.txt), [ThinkGPT package metadata](https://pypi.org/pypi/thinkgpt/0.0.6/json).

Use one real public runtime step instead of the CLI's unlimited retry loop:

```python
from miniagi import MiniAGI
from exceptions import InvalidLLMResponseError

agent = MiniAGI("gpt-4", "gpt-3.5-turbo", "Reply with one short sentence.", 4000, 2000)
try:
    agent.think()
except InvalidLLMResponseError:
    pass  # A plain dummy answer is not the agent's action grammar.
```

Those models and memory budgets match `.env_example`. The constructor and `think()` build the real agent context and invoke ThinkGPT, which wraps the action prompt in its normal context chain and emits OpenAI Chat Completions. Empty initial memory avoids an embedding request before this first step. Commands such as `execute_python`, `execute_shell` and `web_search` are expressed in the text action grammar, not JSON tool schemas; zero JSON tools is correct. Do not run `act()` or rewrite the native prompt. [Agent and action grammar](https://github.com/muellerberndt/mini-agi/blob/f012aa41e46a7b7dea99d1358305f2fb605a6599/miniagi.py), [example settings](https://github.com/muellerberndt/mini-agi/blob/f012aa41e46a7b7dea99d1358305f2fb605a6599/.env_example).

### OpenManus native main entry

Investigated current official commit `3309bf4e416fb1c74b008f3e86494439a31bad53`, rather than treating its old `v0.3.0` release as current source. Install the checkout's requirements and run `python main.py --prompt TEXT`. `main.py` calls `Manus.create()`, `agent.run()` and cleanup. Configuration is fixed at `<checkout>/config/config.toml`, with workspace/logs also rooted in the checkout; use a separate source copy per capture, since HOME alone does not isolate these. [Native entry](https://github.com/FoundationAgents/OpenManus/blob/3309bf4e416fb1c74b008f3e86494439a31bad53/main.py), [configuration paths](https://github.com/FoundationAgents/OpenManus/blob/3309bf4e416fb1c74b008f3e86494439a31bad53/app/config.py), [requirements](https://github.com/FoundationAgents/OpenManus/blob/3309bf4e416fb1c74b008f3e86494439a31bad53/requirements.txt).

Its normal LLM class uses `AsyncOpenAI` for the standard lane. Although the checked-in example names Claude 3.7 and an Anthropic URL, that is not native Anthropic Messages serialization. Prefer a named configured-OpenAI lane with this isolated `config/config.toml`:

```toml
[llm]
model = "gpt-4o"
base_url = "https://api.openai.com/v1"
api_key = "phistory-fake-api-key"
max_tokens = 8192
temperature = 0.0

[daytona]
daytona_api_key = "phistory-fake-daytona-key"
```

Linux validation exposed an upstream configuration requirement: `Config._load_initial_config()` constructs `DaytonaSettings()` even for local Manus, while `daytona_api_key` has no default. The explicit fake `[daytona]` value satisfies that Pydantic field without contacting Daytona. Native PythonExecute is local multiprocessing; StrReplaceEditor uses LocalFileOperator because `sandbox.use_sandbox` defaults false. Manus construction does not initialize a Daytona client. [Required field and unconditional loader](https://github.com/FoundationAgents/OpenManus/blob/3309bf4e416fb1c74b008f3e86494439a31bad53/app/config.py), [local Python tool](https://github.com/FoundationAgents/OpenManus/blob/3309bf4e416fb1c74b008f3e86494439a31bad53/app/tool/python_execute.py), [editor operator selection](https://github.com/FoundationAgents/OpenManus/blob/3309bf4e416fb1c74b008f3e86494439a31bad53/app/tool/str_replace_editor.py).

The native tool collection begins with PythonExecute, StrReplaceEditor, AskHuman and Terminate. Current Manus additionally attempts `uvx browser-use --cli-mcp`, then adds that server's real tools and system instructions; configured MCP servers are also initialized. Preserve this attempt for a full default surface, pre-stage public dependencies in Linux, and archive the actual connected toolset. Connection errors are caught and leave the four core tools available. The explicitly supported `OPENMANUS_DISABLE_BROWSER_USE=1` is a separate local-core variant, not evidence for the full browser MCP surface. Native `max_steps=20` bounds the run. [LLM serializer](https://github.com/FoundationAgents/OpenManus/blob/3309bf4e416fb1c74b008f3e86494439a31bad53/app/llm.py), [actual tool collection and MCP startup](https://github.com/FoundationAgents/OpenManus/blob/3309bf4e416fb1c74b008f3e86494439a31bad53/app/agent/manus.py).

### CUGA 0.4.5 official SDK example

Official PyPI latest is `cuga==0.4.5`; GitHub's latest tagged release remains `v0.4.4`. ### AutoResearchClaw 0.5.0 bounded pipeline

Install official source `aiming-lab/AutoResearchClaw` at `v0.5.0` with Python `>=3.11`, using `pip install -e CHECKOUT`. Its project distribution name is `researchclaw`, version 0.5.0, but public PyPI `researchclaw==0.1.4` is a different project; installing that package would capture the wrong product. No published `autoresearchclaw` package was found. Use the checked-in `config.researchclaw.example.yaml`, fake `OPENAI_API_KEY`, and isolated output/source working state:

```text
researchclaw run --config CONFIG --topic TEXT --output RUN_DIR --to-stage TOPIC_INIT --skip-preflight --auto-approve
```

`--skip-preflight` skips the connectivity ping, not the task. The first actual pipeline stage renders `PromptManager.for_stage("topic_init", ...)` and invokes `LLMClient.chat()` with system and user content, then stops at the supported `--to-stage` boundary before literature search or experiments. The example's native primary is `gpt-4o`, fallbacks `gpt-4.1`/`gpt-4o-mini`, OpenAI-compatible Chat Completions. On CPU Linux the stage also records hardware detection; GPU setup can install PyTorch, so use the normal CPU runner for this bounded capture. [Official project manifest](https://github.com/aiming-lab/AutoResearchClaw/blob/v0.5.0/pyproject.toml), [native CLI boundaries](https://github.com/aiming-lab/AutoResearchClaw/blob/v0.5.0/researchclaw/cli.py), [stage implementation](https://github.com/aiming-lab/AutoResearchClaw/blob/v0.5.0/researchclaw/pipeline/stage_impls/_topic.py), [example config](https://github.com/aiming-lab/AutoResearchClaw/blob/v0.5.0/config.researchclaw.example.yaml).

This stage's `LLMClient.chat()` has no tools parameter; its full prompt is a valid tool-free pipeline surface. The separate MCP catalog exposes ResearchClaw operations to an outer host and must not be inserted into this stage's request. Later code-generation/back-end agent surfaces can be captured additionally, with their own prerequisites and identities. [Native LLM request contract](https://github.com/aiming-lab/AutoResearchClaw/blob/v0.5.0/researchclaw/llm/client.py), [host-facing MCP tools](https://github.com/aiming-lab/AutoResearchClaw/blob/v0.5.0/researchclaw/mcp/tools.py).

### Agent Zero native context API

Follow-up raw run `44114630751` reached the actual Agent 0 request through a **real run_ui RFC worker while preserving development mode**; no Docker-mode flag was spoofed on the host. The native Manual preset emitted main model `openai/gpt-6.1-sol`, a 37,668-character primary prompt and zero JSON tools (native textual tool protocol). It subsequently passed independent fresh validation as recorded above. The earlier naming-only artifact remains diagnostic.

Linux run `44107474846` initially produced only the auxiliary chat-naming request; its main monologue stopped with `No RFC password, cannot handle RFC calls.` It is therefore diagnostic, not a primary-agent capture. Native `helpers.runtime.initialize()` must run before context initialization, and the normal runtime topology must be supplied: `is_development()` means absence of the parsed `dockerized` argument, regardless of OS. For a real Linux runtime container, invoke the driver with `--dockerized=true`, matching production bootstrap; native development functions then execute locally, without removing prompt or tool extensions. For a supported development topology, run the normal `run_ui.py` server with `RFC_PASSWORD`, set `rfc_url=127.0.0.1` and `rfc_port_http` to its listening port, and keep RFC enabled. Its own `/api/rfc` dispatch then supplies the root-password/environment calls. Calling `initialize()` alone leaves development mode active. [Runtime routing and initialization](https://github.com/agent0ai/agent-zero/blob/ba9b67df0f54ac78106ae85c552ef5d2458f7c9a/helpers/runtime.py#L24), [production launch mode](https://github.com/agent0ai/agent-zero/blob/ba9b67df0f54ac78106ae85c552ef5d2458f7c9a/docker/run/fs/exe/self_update_manager.py#L958), [native RFC exchange](https://github.com/agent0ai/agent-zero/blob/ba9b67df0f54ac78106ae85c552ef5d2458f7c9a/helpers/rfc_exchange.py), [RFC endpoint](https://github.com/agent0ai/agent-zero/blob/ba9b67df0f54ac78106ae85c552ef5d2458f7c9a/api/rfc.py).

Pin official source `ba9b67df0f54ac78106ae85c552ef5d2458f7c9a`, install its complete pinned requirements with a suitable Python 3.12 Linux environment, and preserve the checkout's plugins/prompts/resources. The public context API is the same one used by the HTTP message handler:

```python
import asyncio
from helpers import runtime
runtime.initialize()
from agent import AgentContext, UserMessage
from initialize import initialize_agent

async def main():
    context = AgentContext(initialize_agent())
    task = context.communicate(UserMessage(message="Reply with one short sentence."))
    await task.result()

asyncio.run(main())
```

This creates Agent 0 and invokes native history, monologue, system-prompt and tool extensions. It does not require the web UI or a hand-assembled prompt. Use an isolated source copy because `usr/` settings, projects, logs and presets are checkout-relative. Native model presets are now owned by `_model_config`, not the old three model fields in AgentConfig. Before interception, run the official `BootstrapModelPresets(None).execute()` startup extension, imported with `importlib.import_module("plugins._model_config.extensions.python.startup_migration._20_bootstrap_model_presets")`; it downloads the bounded public preset collection, validates and persists it, with the checked-in offline fallback when unavailable. Record the actual preset file/hash and selected Default. [Native API caller](https://github.com/agent0ai/agent-zero/blob/ba9b67df0f54ac78106ae85c552ef5d2458f7c9a/api/message.py), [context and monologue](https://github.com/agent0ai/agent-zero/blob/ba9b67df0f54ac78106ae85c552ef5d2458f7c9a/agent.py), [model-preset bootstrap](https://github.com/agent0ai/agent-zero/blob/ba9b67df0f54ac78106ae85c552ef5d2458f7c9a/plugins/_model_config/extensions/python/startup_migration/_20_bootstrap_model_presets.py), [preset contract](https://github.com/agent0ai/agent-zero/blob/ba9b67df0f54ac78106ae85c552ef5d2458f7c9a/plugins/_model_config/README.md).

The offline Default currently uses OpenRouter `openai/gpt-5.6-terra` for chat, `google/gemini-3.1-flash-lite` for utility, and local `sentence-transformers/all-MiniLM-L6-v2` embeddings. Supply only fake credentials matching the **resolved** online/default preset; do not silently label the offline collection as the online default. Preserve memory recall and other prompt extensions; pre-cache required local embedding weights in Linux setup so public weight downloads are not replaced with a model dummy. `A0_SET_MAX_CONSECUTIVE_UNUSABLE_RESPONSES=1` is a supported optional bound for plain dummy replies. Its complete dependency set includes FAISS, speech and local-model libraries, so installation/cache capacity is a concrete prerequisite rather than a reason to replace the runtime with templates. [Fallback preset](https://github.com/agent0ai/agent-zero/blob/ba9b67df0f54ac78106ae85c552ef5d2458f7c9a/plugins/_model_config/mode_presets_fallback.yaml), [credential/model construction](https://github.com/agent0ai/agent-zero/blob/ba9b67df0f54ac78106ae85c552ef5d2458f7c9a/models.py), [settings and termination bound](https://github.com/agent0ai/agent-zero/blob/ba9b67df0f54ac78106ae85c552ef5d2458f7c9a/helpers/settings.py).

### AgenticSeek configured coder API

Pin current official source `8f705be8ca625e9549389f188b1e37f700d3ed34`; there is no published release to substitute. Install its requirements in a compatible CPU Python environment and use an isolated source copy/HOME/workspace. The interactive CLI starts Chromium, speech/session management and a router, but its actual coder has a direct native task API:

```python
import asyncio
from sources.llm_provider import Provider
from sources.agents.code_agent import CoderAgent

provider = Provider("openai", "gpt-4.1", is_local=False)
agent = CoderAgent("coder", "prompts/jarvis/coder_agent.txt", provider, verbose=False)
asyncio.run(agent.process("Reply with one short sentence.", None))
```

Linux startup additionally requires the normal `libportaudio2` system library: importing `sources.agents` eagerly imports Planner/speech and sounddevice, even for this coder-only task. The first CI attempt failed on that missing library before any request. Installing the dependency preserves upstream imports; no speech mock or source patch is appropriate. Set supported `AGENT_RUNTIME_DIR` and `WORK_DIR` before imports so mutable paths remain within the private run.

Supply fake `OPENAI_API_KEY`. This is an explicitly configured OpenAI coder surface; the checked-in CLI defaults are Ollama `qwen3.8:27b` and Jarvis personality. The API constructs the real coder memory, system instructions and six built-in code/file tools; `wait_message(None)` explicitly skips speech. A normal plain dummy has no fenced code and returns after one model call. Its native tools use fenced text/code blocks rather than JSON function schemas. `prompts/base/coder_agent.txt` is another supported persona, not the default Jarvis prompt. Browser/router/audio/model-download startup is avoided by choosing this genuine domain-agent API, without changing that agent's prompt construction or tool registration. [CLI/defaults](https://github.com/Fosowl/agenticSeek/blob/8f705be8ca625e9549389f188b1e37f700d3ed34/config.ini), [coder task and tools](https://github.com/Fosowl/agenticSeek/blob/8f705be8ca625e9549389f188b1e37f700d3ed34/sources/agents/code_agent.py), [native memory/request and optional speech](https://github.com/Fosowl/agenticSeek/blob/8f705be8ca625e9549389f188b1e37f700d3ed34/sources/agents/agent.py), [OpenAI serializer](https://github.com/Fosowl/agenticSeek/blob/8f705be8ca625e9549389f188b1e37f700d3ed34/sources/llm_provider.py).

Set `AGENT_RUNTIME_DIR=STATE/runtime` and `WORK_DIR=WORKDIR`; those are the native supported paths for logs/session data and the tool workspace. Otherwise workspace defaults to `<cwd>/.agent-data/workspace`. The configured OpenAI coder's request is nonstreaming Chat Completions, and source `Provider` performs no API verification at construction. [Workspace resolution](https://github.com/Fosowl/agenticSeek/blob/8f705be8ca625e9549389f188b1e37f700d3ed34/sources/workspace.py).

### ZCode Linux source CLI

Latest official desktop release `v3.18.2` has macOS/Windows assets, with no Linux CLI asset. The official source at `fec975905a2abd4c07e35c40d51fa097a2258ec9` contains the Linux-buildable CLI; its workspace manifest declares 0.17.1 and internal private package `@zcode/cli` declares 0.1.0, so record the actual compiled `--version` and source commit rather than assigning the desktop release number to that CLI. Use Node 24.14.0 and pnpm 10.33.2, run root `pnpm install --frozen-lockfile` followed by `pnpm --filter @zcode/cli... build`, and retain the entire built checkout/resources. The normal task is `node apps/zcode-cli/packages/cli/dist/zcode.cjs --prompt TEXT --cwd WORKDIR --json`. Copying just `zcode.cjs` loses bundled plugins, workers and native resources. Do not substitute third-party `zcode-app-cli` or unrelated npm stubs. [Official releases](https://github.com/zai-org/ZCode/releases/tag/v3.18.2), [source build](https://github.com/zai-org/ZCode/blob/fec975905a2abd4c07e35c40d51fa097a2258ec9/README.md), [CLI workspace manifest](https://github.com/zai-org/ZCode/blob/fec975905a2abd4c07e35c40d51fa097a2258ec9/apps/zcode-cli/package.json), [CLI package](https://github.com/zai-org/ZCode/blob/fec975905a2abd4c07e35c40d51fa097a2258ec9/apps/zcode-cli/packages/cli/package.json).

Use isolated HOME, `ZCODE_DATA_BASE_DIR`, `ZCODE_STORAGE_DIR`, `ZCODE_SESSION_DB_PATH`, and `ZCODE_LOG_DIR`. Point `ZCODE_PERSONAL_PROVIDER_CONFIG_FILE` at this normal provider configuration containing only the fake key:

```json
{"schemaVersion":1,"config":{"providerConfigRules":{"providerRules":[{"providerId":"phistory","providerName":"Phistory API","config":{"group":"standard-personal","access":{"type":"api-key","apiKey":"phistory-fake-api-key"},"api":{"type":"openai-chat-completions","baseUrl":"https://api.openai.com/v1"},"personalModelIds":["gpt-4.1"]}}]},"modelConfigRules":{"providerModelRules":[],"manualProviderModelRules":[]},"defaultModelSelection":{"providerId":"phistory","modelId":"gpt-4.1"}}}
```

This is a configured model lane, with ordinary feature defaults retained. Its docs' disabled-extension benchmark config is explicitly not the normal default. Native `--prompt` defaults to yolo mode; `--model`, `--settings`, `--print` and `--max-turns` are unsupported flags. In the capture wrapper, map tap's `HTTP_PROXY` to native `ZCODE_HTTP_PROXY` and `SSL_CERT_FILE` to `ZCODE_AGENT_CA_CERT` before exec, since provider runtime process environment forwarding has its own contract. The personal provider file contains literal values; it does not promise `${ENV}` interpolation. ## Sixth runtime batch: native application and task APIs

The following eight contracts are source/package inspections awaiting Linux capture. The drivers invoke project-owned task execution and retain native prompt/tool construction. ### BambooAI native free-tier quick analysis

Official PyPI `bambooai==2.7.4` matches release `v2.7.4` and requires Python >=3.11. Its package includes `web_app`, `analyst`, the template and native code executor, so a public SDK invocation can exercise the same analyst session without deploying Flask or Docker. Initialize a private working home and build its model configuration with the shipped builder, then enter the actual analysis method:

```python
import os
from bambooai.cli import init_home, load_env
from web_app.llm_config_builder import build_user_config
from bambooai import BambooAI

init_home(STATE, quiet=True)
load_env(STATE)
os.chdir(STATE)
assert build_user_config("phistory")
BambooAI(user_id="phistory").pd_agent_converse(question=TEXT, mode="quick")
```

Set isolated HOME, fake `OPENROUTER_API_KEY`, and optionally explicit `BAMBOO_DATA_DIR`/`BAMBOO_MEMORY_DIR` beneath STATE before imports. `build_user_config` defaults to the real free tier and writes `config/phistory/LLM_CONFIG.json`; no subscription identity is invented. Although its comment still says “Groq”, the release's actual `free_agent_configs` use OpenRouter `deepseek/deepseek-v4.1-flash-1017` for Analyst and most other seats, with Gemini `gemini-flash-latest` for Google Search Executor. Preserve all seats. A fake Gemini key is only needed if that search seat is actually invoked. Name this `sdk-free-quick`; the single-user CLI application's cost preference is a separate configuration. [Official package](https://pypi.org/project/bambooai/2.7.4/), [packaged modules](https://github.com/pgalko/BambooAI/blob/v2.7.4/pyproject.toml), [home bootstrap](https://github.com/pgalko/BambooAI/blob/v2.7.4/bambooai/cli.py), [native configuration builder](https://github.com/pgalko/BambooAI/blob/v2.7.4/web_app/llm_config_builder.py), [actual seat template](https://github.com/pgalko/BambooAI/blob/v2.7.4/web_app/LLM_CONFIG_template.json).

The constructor's native default execution mode is local, its kernel is lazy, and `pd_agent_converse` constructs its own Notebook, Session and Budget. `quick`, `deep` and `adaptive` are native budgets of the same session. Provider preflight imports configured SDKs but does not validate keys by generating. Retain the full session and its text/code tools rather than building a prompt from `analyst/contract.md` directly. [Native constructor and task method](https://github.com/pgalko/BambooAI/blob/v2.7.4/bambooai/bambooai.py), [configuration and import preflight](https://github.com/pgalko/BambooAI/blob/v2.7.4/bambooai/models/__init__.py).

### PR-Agent native plain-diff review

Install official PyPI `pr-agent==0.50.0`, Python >=3.12. The matching Git tag's manifest still declares 0.49.0; record the installed wheel metadata rather than silently deriving its version from that stale field. Use a small, real UTF-8 unified diff as task input and the native CLI:

pr-agent --diff-file INPUT.diff --output STATE/review.md --json-output STATE/review.json review

Its publishing is local stdout/output files, so no GitHub token, remote PR or comment is required. ### Ouroboros managed headless task

Linux 7.11.0 startup reached the root page before the supervisor could accept a task (`503 supervisor is still starting`). Ordinary CLI `dialogue_ready` is **insufficient for managed tasks**, as a subsequent Linux trial confirmed. Poll **GET `/api/health`** and require **`boot.tasks_ready is True`**; health_contracts.py explicitly distinguishes durable owner-dialogue admission from pooled-task admission. The task controller separately requires supervisor_ready_event and an available worker pool. Fail immediately on boot.phase failed or child-process exit, retain final boot/step JSON at a wall deadline, and submit the task once after task readiness. A 0.5-second polling cadence matches the native ordinary-dialogue wait; use the stronger task field rather than blind sleep, a phase-string guess or repeated task probes. Exact tag v7.11.0 resolves to `4be731ffcd2990acff55fb746b0daa073c22d5af`. [Native readiness distinction](https://github.com/razzant/ouroboros/blob/v7.11.0/ouroboros/gateway/health_contracts.py#L43), [actual managed-task gate](https://github.com/razzant/ouroboros/blob/v7.11.0/ouroboros/gateway/tasks.py#L1531), [ordinary CLI wait](https://github.com/razzant/ouroboros/blob/v7.11.0/ouroboros/cli.py#L788).

Use official `razzant/ouroboros` release `v7.11.0` source, with its documented uv installation or pinned checkout and lock-resolved environment. Public PyPI package `ouroboros` belongs to another project and is unsuitable. The actual normal headless task is:

```sh
ouroboros run --start --workspace WORKDIR --jsonl --timeout 60 --execution-timeout 60 TEXT
```

For owned process cleanup, start `ouroboros server --host 127.0.0.1 --port PORT --no-ui` as a child inside the capture wrapper and run `ouroboros --url http://127.0.0.1:PORT run --workspace WORKDIR --jsonl --timeout 60 --execution-timeout 60 TEXT`; terminate the owned server afterward. `--start` otherwise starts a background server with detached output. Preserve a writable source checkout, private HOME, `OUROBOROS_DATA_DIR` and `OUROBOROS_APP_ROOT`, and fake `OPENROUTER_API_KEY`. Map tap's CA file to native `OUROBOROS_EXTRA_CA_BUNDLE` if its first-party transport requires explicit additional trust. [Official source install](https://github.com/razzant/ouroboros/blob/v7.11.0/README.md#run-from-source), [actual task/server/timeout arguments](https://github.com/razzant/ouroboros/blob/v7.11.0/ouroboros/cli.py).

Native defaults with OpenRouter access are main `google/gemini-3.8-flash`, light `openai/gpt-6-luna`, fallback `openai/gpt-5.6-luna`; heavy/vision/consciousness inherit through their empty native settings. The managed task POSTs to `/api/tasks` with external workspace and default forked memory, then awaits the normal task/event path. Startup checks configured access, not a successful generation. Settings reads merge an otherwise absent key from the environment, so fake access can be supplied without fabricating an onboarding-complete marker. Startup/catalog, custody and supervisor effects still require Linux observation; keep them under tap and do not claim a successful capture from source inspection alone. [Defaults](https://github.com/razzant/ouroboros/blob/v7.11.0/ouroboros/settings_defaults.py), [settings environment merging](https://github.com/razzant/ouroboros/blob/v7.11.0/ouroboros/config.py#L829), [startup-ready gate](https://github.com/razzant/ouroboros/blob/v7.11.0/ouroboros/server_runtime.py#L278), [configured connection test](https://github.com/razzant/ouroboros/blob/v7.11.0/ouroboros/provider_connections.py#L133).

### localGPT native Ollama generate task

Pin official source `34f3107f56a88203859092312231b945a711c619`, install its requirements, and run `python -m rag_system.main chat TEXT` from an isolated source/work directory. Native backend is Ollama, generation model `qwen3.5:9b`, enrichment/initial triage model `qwen3.5:4b`. Set only the supported transport host `OLLAMA_HOST=http://ollama.phistory.invalid`, plus private HOME/cache and `LANCEDB_PATH`, to route the actual native request into forward capture; no provider key or local Ollama server is needed behind the dummy. [CLI and defaults](https://github.com/PromtEngineer/localGPT/blob/34f3107f56a88203859092312231b945a711c619/rag_system/main.py), [real factory](https://github.com/PromtEngineer/localGPT/blob/34f3107f56a88203859092312231b945a711c619/rag_system/factory.py), [requirements](https://github.com/PromtEngineer/localGPT/blob/34f3107f56a88203859092312231b945a711c619/requirements.txt).

This client sends **POST `/api/generate`**, not `/api/chat`: `{model,prompt,stream:false,...}` contains the flattened native prompt. Tap needs the genuine generate response shape, for example `response`, `done`, `prompt_eval_count`, `eval_count`, and exporter support for the flat prompt. A chat/SSE dummy is insufficient. The native Agent sends its triage prompt first; database, text embedder and reranker construction is explicitly deferred, so that first generation does not require downloading embedding weights or an existing document index. Later RAG stages can need both; label a triage capture accordingly. JSON tools are absent on this initial native surface. [Generate wire protocol](https://github.com/PromtEngineer/localGPT/blob/34f3107f56a88203859092312231b945a711c619/rag_system/utils/ollama_client.py), [native task loop](https://github.com/PromtEngineer/localGPT/blob/34f3107f56a88203859092312231b945a711c619/rag_system/agent/loop.py), [lazy retrieval initialization](https://github.com/PromtEngineer/localGPT/blob/34f3107f56a88203859092312231b945a711c619/rag_system/pipelines/retrieval_pipeline.py#L54).

from config import load_config

Although the signature/docstring permits `None`, Linux execution showed a later unguarded `context.get("delegated_permission_mode")` fails before Agent initialization; the earlier source-only assumption that `None` was runnable was incorrect. The native Context preserves normal default-profile routing. Private HOME isolates its default `~/cow` workspace; `COW_DATA_DIR` separately owns mutable config/data. This API runs AgentInitializer, workspace templates, context files, skills, memory and PromptBuilder before the actual Agent loop. It avoids a channel server/login by selecting the real domain task API, not by assembling a prompt separately. Memory initialization tries the native default embedding provider and contains a caught failure path; do not disable memory/tools merely to simplify capture. Fresh workspace indexing, scheduler and evolution startup can emit auxiliary requests, so include the entire process inside tap and retain those requests separately from the main Agent request. Any failure that removes memory tools must be recorded rather than called complete default coverage. [Memory initialization and failure handling](https://github.com/zhayujie/CowAgent/blob/2.2.2/bridge/agent_initializer.py#L831), [native AgentBridge routing](https://github.com/zhayujie/CowAgent/blob/2.2.2/bridge/agent_bridge.py#L1510).

Current-source follow-up verified `bcc36de52ca17b45d1132f388a04b7a456bd3f95` against tag `2.2.2` (`8c90b740f58c7a3ca6931b1f0c0287192bba9650`). agent-qa run tests/phistory.yaml --headless --disable-cache --no-parallel --detach
```

`--detach` skips waiting for the post-run knowledge-curation tail; use a bounded owned process for the main run. This invokes real Playwright state/screenshot and the native planner's system/tool construction. Its compatible OpenAI adapter explicitly uses Chat Completions (`createOpenAI(...).chat(modelId)`), so existing typed dummy transport can capture the first complete request even if a plain dummy cannot satisfy the planner's structured answer. No hosted QA account is required. Outer `OPENAI_API_KEY` alone is insufficient: the inspected native resolver reads the named credential store. ### DeepAnalyze native local-serving client

Pin official source `afc697a11675c59683b9f6596c3ab3b3d661182a`; there is no release/package to replace this source client. The actual `deepanalyze.py` class uses requests and the standard library. ```python
from deepanalyze import DeepAnalyzeVLLM
DeepAnalyzeVLLM(
    "DeepAnalyze-8B",
    api_url="http://deepanalyze.phistory.invalid/v1/chat/completions",
    max_rounds=1,

```

`DeepAnalyze-8B` is the README's documented vLLM served-model alias; native endpoint defaults to localhost:8000. This named local-serving client surface needs no fake API key, GPU or model server behind capture-only dummy responses. Its actual initial body contains model, a **single user message**, temperature 0.5, max_tokens 32768, `add_generation_prompt=false` and `stop=["</Code>"]`. The client supplies neither a system message nor JSON tools; record that absence faithfully. Tool use is its native `<Code>`/execution-message loop, and later code execution only happens after actual model-produced code. A plain dummy can end the bounded first round, but this first request does not cover later code/answer rounds. [Official client](https://github.com/ruc-datalab/DeepAnalyze/blob/afc697a11675c59683b9f6596c3ab3b3d661182a/deepanalyze.py), [normal serving and client examples](https://github.com/ruc-datalab/DeepAnalyze/blob/afc697a11675c59683b9f6596c3ab3b3d661182a/README.md), [requirements](https://github.com/ruc-datalab/DeepAnalyze/blob/afc697a11675c59683b9f6596c3ab3b3d661182a/requirements.txt).

### Hive native Queen session

The repository root is a uv workspace placeholder: `pip install -e .` there does not install the actual framework. Use the documented workspace setup or `aden-hive[tools]==1.0.12` with the installed version recorded separately; for current-source semantics, retain the whole source workspace and its tools. [Official installation warning](https://github.com/aden-hive/hive/blob/beb6c3dd7c37ffb09b7a38adeb5eb6f1f486ccd2/README.md#quick-start), [framework package](https://github.com/aden-hive/hive/blob/beb6c3dd7c37ffb09b7a38adeb5eb6f1f486ccd2/core/pyproject.toml), [PyPI channel](https://pypi.org/project/aden-hive/1.0.12/).

Set private HOME and `HIVE_HOME=STATE`, and write `STATE/configuration.json` containing `{"llm":{"api_key":"phistory-fake-api-key"}}`. Literal `llm.api_key` is a supported native credential source. With no model selection it preserves the framework's actual preferred default `anthropic/claude-sonnet-5-5`. Alternatively create the session first and call `hive chat SESSION TEXT --server http://127.0.0.1:PORT`; that normal CLI POSTs `/api/sessions/{id}/chat`. ### IntentKit local native Lead API

For the reference application, install the production dependency **group** with `uv sync --no-default-groups --group app`. The core package alone omits `sentry_sdk`, which `app.api` imports; Linux startup confirmed that omission before any model request. The native `app` group also supplies uvicorn, gunicorn, settings, multipart, JWT and PDF runtime dependencies. [Production app dependency group](https://github.com/crestalnetwork/intentkit/blob/afc8cc44255210db6352f7437e569ed8b88ed5e1/pyproject.toml#L174).

Official PyPI `intentkit==2.6.3` requires Python **3.13.x**. Current source `afc8cc44255210db6352f7437e569ed8b88ed5e1` also includes the reference application needed for the simplest supported task route. Use its source/locked app dependencies and real isolated PostgreSQL and Redis. Set `ENV=local`, `DB_HOST`, `DB_PORT`, `DB_USERNAME`, `DB_PASSWORD`, `DB_NAME`, `DB_AUTO_MIGRATE=true`, `REDIS_HOST`, `REDIS_PORT`, and fake `DEEPSEEK_API_KEY`, leaving other model credentials unset. Start `uvicorn app.api:app --host 127.0.0.1 --port PORT` inside tap. [Official package](https://pypi.org/project/intentkit/2.6.3/), [source manifest](https://github.com/crestalnetwork/intentkit/blob/afc8cc44255210db6352f7437e569ed8b88ed5e1/pyproject.toml), [native service settings](https://github.com/crestalnetwork/intentkit/blob/afc8cc44255210db6352f7437e569ed8b88ed5e1/.env.example), [compose service dependencies](https://github.com/crestalnetwork/intentkit/blob/afc8cc44255210db6352f7437e569ed8b88ed5e1/docker-compose.yml).

This is the explicitly unauthenticated single-user local API, not the team-facing JWT service. Its lifespan performs normal database/Redis initialization, creates the real `system` user/team, and warms the native public-agent/tool catalog. POST `/lead/chats` with `{}` to obtain the chat ID, then POST `/lead/chats/{id}/messages` with `{"message":TEXT,"stream":false}`. No cloud identity, funded wallet or fake team DTO is needed. Native `execute_lead` builds the Lead from database state and calls the actual dynamic prompt/tool executor; with the above available provider, its ranked native first choice is `deepseek-flash`. Optional object-store bootstrap returns when no S3 client/bucket is configured. [Local application lifecycle and system records](https://github.com/crestalnetwork/intentkit/blob/afc8cc44255210db6352f7437e569ed8b88ed5e1/app/api.py), [native Lead endpoints](https://github.com/crestalnetwork/intentkit/blob/afc8cc44255210db6352f7437e569ed8b88ed5e1/app/local/lead.py), [local message schema](https://github.com/crestalnetwork/intentkit/blob/afc8cc44255210db6352f7437e569ed8b88ed5e1/app/common/chat.py#L94), [Lead executor](https://github.com/crestalnetwork/intentkit/blob/afc8cc44255210db6352f7437e569ed8b88ed5e1/intentkit/core/lead/engine.py), [native model ranking](https://github.com/crestalnetwork/intentkit/blob/afc8cc44255210db6352f7437e569ed8b88ed5e1/intentkit/models/llm_picker.py#L95).

The native contract defines all as every capability and states that existing boundary procedures normally use it unless they already have a narrower contract. **Verified runtime:** the complete official v0.11.3 local appliance's genuine baseline chat passed [initial and independent fresh44148244880](https://github.com/DragonnZhang/phistory/actions/runs/44148244880): native anthropic/claude-sonnet-5-5,29,900 identical system characters and20 identical native tools. Latest platform release is `autogpt-platform-beta-v0.11.3`; current inspected source is `657153887ea25aca92014ee713f9ceb344378e74`. The real surveyed baseline task function is `stream_chat_completion_baseline(session_id, message=TEXT, ...)`, backed by a real persisted ChatSession. It constructs the platform's native system prompt/tool registry and calls the OpenAI-compatible client. Default configuration enables the Claude Agent SDK, so choose the explicitly supported baseline profile with `CHAT_USE_CLAUDE_AGENT_SDK=false`, fake `CHAT_API_KEY` or `OPEN_ROUTER_API_KEY`, and native `CHAT_USE_OPENROUTER=true`. Its native baseline standard model is `anthropic/claude-sonnet-5-5`; no forced model change is needed. E2B provisioning is conditional on an active E2B key, so an unset key does not require an E2B account before the first baseline request. [Native baseline](https://github.com/Significant-Gravitas/AutoGPT/blob/657153887ea25aca92014ee713f9ceb344378e74/autogpt_platform/backend/backend/copilot/baseline/service.py#L1862), [engine/provider/model settings](https://github.com/Significant-Gravitas/AutoGPT/blob/657153887ea25aca92014ee713f9ceb344378e74/autogpt_platform/backend/backend/copilot/config.py).

The supported full Linux deployment is the official `significantgravitas/autogpt:v0.11.3` single-container image, with a private data volume, real bundled PostgreSQL/RabbitMQ/three-node Valkey/FalkorDB and loopback application port. Its documented startup/steady-state footprint is roughly 5–6 GiB, so it needs a dedicated heavy job. Local registration is open by default and email verification defaults false. **The image's public backend prefix is /_agpt/**: after native Better Auth registration/token issuance, use Bearer `POST /_agpt/api/chat/sessions` with `{}`, read returned **id**, then `POST /_agpt/api/chat/sessions/ID/stream` with `{"message":TEXT}`. StreamChatRequest does not declare a mode field; select baseline through the real CHAT_USE_CLAUDE_AGENT_SDK=false configuration, rather than expecting an extra body mode to choose an engine. Native POST /api/chat/... is the internal REST route, not the public container path. See the exact container/auth contract appended below. This is a concrete full-stack dependency, not a requirement for a paid hosted AutoGPT account. ### MIRIX native SQLite memory-agent server

Pin official source `9077e4b229dd8f61c5703b431a97788c6b61acfb`, install its Python >=3.10 source requirements, and use private HOME/MIRIX_DIR. Public PyPI `mirix` is 0.1.7 while the source manifest declares 0.1.0; public `mirix-client` currently returns 404 despite the README, so do not substitute an assumed client package. Start the real app with `uvicorn mirix.server.rest_api:app --host 127.0.0.1 --port PORT`. The app lifespan performs normal initialization. Current `mirix.__main__` only accepts `db-maintenance`; the Docker script's `mirix server` is stale and should not be used for this source lane. [Source manifest](https://github.com/Mirix-AI/MIRIX/blob/9077e4b229dd8f61c5703b431a97788c6b61acfb/pyproject.toml), [actual FastAPI lifecycle](https://github.com/Mirix-AI/MIRIX/blob/9077e4b229dd8f61c5703b431a97788c6b61acfb/mirix/server/rest_api.py#L290), [actual module entrypoint](https://github.com/Mirix-AI/MIRIX/blob/9077e4b229dd8f61c5703b431a97788c6b61acfb/mirix/__main__.py).

With PG/PGlite environment unset, the server has a real `sqlite+aiosqlite` fallback at its native recall-storage path; Redis defaults false. No fake database or mandatory PostgreSQL is needed. Prewarm its normal tiktoken assets before tap. First POST `/admin/auth/register` with `{name,email,password}` creates the native initial admin without auth and returns a local JWT. Use that bearer to POST `/agents/meta/initialize` with `{"config":PARSED_NATIVE_YAML}` from `mirix/configs/mirix.yaml`, preserving its full agent list. Main YAML chat default is OpenAI `gpt-4o-mini`, while its actual embedding default is Google `text-embedding-004`, dimension 768; set fake `OPENAI_API_KEY` and `GEMINI_API_KEY` as needed, preserving these defaults. The separate official all-OpenAI example is a configured variant. [Native SQLite branch](https://github.com/Mirix-AI/MIRIX/blob/9077e4b229dd8f61c5703b431a97788c6b61acfb/mirix/server/server.py#L342), [optional Redis](https://github.com/Mirix-AI/MIRIX/blob/9077e4b229dd8f61c5703b431a97788c6b61acfb/mirix/settings.py#L187), [normal config](https://github.com/Mirix-AI/MIRIX/blob/9077e4b229dd8f61c5703b431a97788c6b61acfb/mirix/configs/mirix.yaml), [initial registration](https://github.com/Mirix-AI/MIRIX/blob/9077e4b229dd8f61c5703b431a97788c6b61acfb/mirix/server/rest_api.py#L7959), [meta-agent initialization](https://github.com/Mirix-AI/MIRIX/blob/9077e4b229dd8f61c5703b431a97788c6b61acfb/mirix/server/rest_api.py#L2184).

Keep the returned meta-agent ID and GET `/agents?parent_id=META_ID`, select the real episodic AgentType/name, and POST `/agents/{id}/messages` with **`{"message":TEXT,"role":"user"}`**. Unfiltered GET `/agents` lists only top-level agents, with their children nested; Linux startup confirmed that scanning only this outer list misses the managed episodic agent. This enters the surveyed memory-agent runtime and its real prompt/tools, without constructing an agent state manually. A full meta ingestion can instead use `/memory/add_sync` with real conversation messages and meta_agent_id; do not set `session_tag="task"`, since that path stores a skill-learning session without invoking the meta memory LLM. Preserve auxiliary requests and note which actual agent emitted each. [Parent-filtered list API](https://github.com/Mirix-AI/MIRIX/blob/9077e4b229dd8f61c5703b431a97788c6b61acfb/mirix/server/rest_api.py#L936), [native top-level/children semantics](https://github.com/Mirix-AI/MIRIX/blob/9077e4b229dd8f61c5703b431a97788c6b61acfb/mirix/services/agent_manager.py#L1253), [synchronous native ingestion and task-only exclusion](https://github.com/Mirix-AI/MIRIX/blob/9077e4b229dd8f61c5703b431a97788c6b61acfb/mirix/server/rest_api.py#L2980).

Set one explicit private `TIKTOKEN_CACHE_DIR` in both asset prewarming and server capture environments, and prewarm `tiktoken.get_encoding("o200k_base")` there before tap. MIRIX otherwise changes the default cache to `<MIRIX_DIR>/tiktoken`; prewarming tiktoken's unrelated default cache does not satisfy its native startup preload and causes a proxy-side asset/hash failure. [Native cache resolver and loader](https://github.com/Mirix-AI/MIRIX/blob/9077e4b229dd8f61c5703b431a97788c6b61acfb/mirix/services/context_budget.py#L281).

Its concrete production task API is `features/chats.createConversation(userId, options)` followed by `features/group_chat.send({userId,cid,text:TEXT})`; send seeds the real reserved actors and enqueues the Commander/bus runtime. The model adapter `model/core-agent/client.streamChatWithModel` builds native sessions/tools and emits model requests, but imports Electron `app` and uses the application's actual persisted BYOK/model/workspace state. A standalone call to the lower `src/core-agent` AgentRunner with an invented prompt/tool registry would omit the surveyed Commander surface. The next driver therefore needs a real initialized Electron main process, private application storage and a source-confirmed normal BYOK/profile bootstrap before calling that production API. No such complete CLI recipe is claimed here; substituting an Electron mock or manually passing chat_commander.md would be invalid. Source discovery established the exact task spine and concrete bootstrap gap, rather than labeling the app a generic prototype. [Conversation creation](https://github.com/Orkas-AI/Orkas/blob/3163c7ef1c2b03e21e6f85c6301515609361c570/src/main/features/chats.ts#L2202), [native Commander send](https://github.com/Orkas-AI/Orkas/blob/3163c7ef1c2b03e21e6f85c6301515609361c570/src/main/features/group_chat/index.ts#L309), [real model adapter and Electron dependency](https://github.com/Orkas-AI/Orkas/blob/3163c7ef1c2b03e21e6f85c6301515609361c570/src/main/model/core-agent/client.ts).

### WeChat Mac RPA portable native reply engine

The full bot at current `31cf55be1264acf35d436e754deda06833ec4087` requires macOS 12+, Mac WeChat, Vision, screen-recording/accessibility permissions; `run_bot.py` is not a valid Linux GUI capture path. Its **surveyed ReplyGenerator is separately portable**: it accepts normal domain ChatMessage inputs and constructs the actual native system prompt, ReAct loop and registry JSON schemas without importing the Mac UI/OCR spine. Use a private source copy/HOME with compatible portable dependencies; the entire requirements file cannot be installed unchanged on Linux because it includes `pyobjc-framework-Vision`. Install the native reply spine's ordinary Python dependencies, including OpenAI, requests, BeautifulSoup, TinyTuya and paho-mqtt, without stubbing the Mac modules. [Documented full-bot OS boundary](https://github.com/wq19901103wq/wechat-mac-rpa/blob/31cf55be1264acf35d436e754deda06833ec4087/README.md#quick-start), [dependency boundary](https://github.com/wq19901103wq/wechat-mac-rpa/blob/31cf55be1264acf35d436e754deda06833ec4087/requirements.txt), [actual reply API](https://github.com/wq19901103wq/wechat-mac-rpa/blob/31cf55be1264acf35d436e754deda06833ec4087/src/reply/generator.py#L758).

```python
from src.tools import register_builtin_tools
from src.utils.qwen_client import QwenClient
from src.reply.generator import ReplyGenerator
from src.models.base import ChatMessage, SenderType

register_builtin_tools()
message = ChatMessage(text=TEXT, sender="Phistory", sender_type=SenderType.OTHER,
                      chat_name="Phistory")
ReplyGenerator(llm_client=QwenClient()).generate([message], [message])
```

Supply fake `DEEPSEEK_API_KEY`; despite the QwenClient class name, its native current model is `deepseek-v4-flash`, endpoint `https://api.deepseek.com/v1`. Keep native `ENABLE_REACT_TOOLS` default on and register the real built-ins before the task. Persona/skills are loaded by the native generator; an isolated fresh source copy uses public defaults rather than a user's personal persona/history. Optional indexed history/memory tools only activate when their native data/dependencies exist, so label this `reply-engine` and record the resulting schemas. It is real domain task input, not fabricated GUI/OCR evidence, and does not open WeChat or send a message. [Current provider defaults](https://github.com/wq19901103wq/wechat-mac-rpa/blob/31cf55be1264acf35d436e754deda06833ec4087/src/utils/qwen_client.py#L26), [native ChatMessage](https://github.com/wq19901103wq/wechat-mac-rpa/blob/31cf55be1264acf35d436e754deda06833ec4087/src/models/base.py), [real built-in registration](https://github.com/wq19901103wq/wechat-mac-rpa/blob/31cf55be1264acf35d436e754deda06833ec4087/src/tools/builtin_tools.py#L248).

This surface genuinely emits native multimodal reasoning messages, with no system message or JSON function schemas on that initial call. Label it initial planner, not a completed full image-refinement run: continuing after the intercepted response can require large public weights and substantial GPU memory. This records source-audit metadata, not a model request or runtime success. In CI, the ordinary configured gateway must relay **only GET /v1/models** to the actual public catalogue and archive its raw bytes/hash, route **POST /v1/chat/completions** unchanged into capture-only tap, and refuse other paths. Preserve order, IDs and metadata; no synthetic catalogue, skipTest or E2E_SKIP_PROVIDER_TEST. This transport boundary leaves the real provider probe, registry reconciliation, Queenie selection and agent assembly intact. The existing generic tap's synthetic grok models response cannot serve this purpose. [Optional-key native connector/probe](https://github.com/MarlBurroW/hivekeep/blob/e6bf9aea4159312730a690b94697084616b44556/src/server/llm/llm/openai-compatible.ts#L633), [native catalogue discovery](https://github.com/MarlBurroW/hivekeep/blob/e6bf9aea4159312730a690b94697084616b44556/src/server/llm/llm/openai-compatible.ts#L662), [dedicated OpenRouter credential distinction](https://github.com/MarlBurroW/hivekeep/blob/e6bf9aea4159312730a690b94697084616b44556/src/server/llm/llm/openrouter.ts#L492), [public upstream catalogue](https://openrouter.ai/api/v1/models).

The real main.ts runs Drizzle migrations, creates SQLite/FTS/vector tables, seeds built-in toolboxes/tools and starts the queue worker. Its encryption key is generated/persisted normally at DATA/.encryption-key if ENCRYPTION_KEY is unset. No system service, external SQL database, SMTP or UI build is needed for the first API task. Wait for GET `/api/onboarding/status`200. Use the normal same-Origin cookie flow:

| Step | Native request and response |
| --- | --- |
| Local identity | POST `/api/auth/sign-up/email` `{email:"phistory@example.com",password:STRONG_PRIVATE_PASSWORD,name:"Phistory"}`. BetterAuth minimum password8; use a generated stronger value. Preserve actual Set-Cookie session values, not caller-minted JWTs. |
| First profile | Cookie POST `/api/onboarding/profile` `{firstName:"Phistory",pseudonym:"phistory",language:"en"}` →201 raw profile with role admin. Only the first fresh profile skips invitations; no profile/ACL shortcut is needed. |
| Native provider | Cookie POST `/api/providers` `{name:"Phistory compatible OpenRouter",type:"openai-compatible",config:{baseUrl:"http://127.0.0.1:GATE/v1",apiKey:FAKE},families:["llm"]}` →201 `{provider:{id,...,isValid:true}}`. Omit skipTest; require its actual valid result. Reconciliation uses the same live catalogue. |
| Primary kickoff | With capture already enabled, cookie POST `/api/onboarding/configurator` `{providerId:ID}` →201 `{agent:{id,slug,name:"Queenie",kind:"configurator"}}`. This immediately enqueues the native onboarding task; it is not only object creation. |
| Subsequent task | Optional cookie POST `/api/agents/ID/messages` `{content:TEXT}` →202 `{messageId,queuePosition}`. The initial Queenie kickoff is already a primary task, so this is not required just to create a request. |

Queenie's primary system marker is `You are Queenie:`; its native kickoff begins `[A new user just finished initial setup` and instructs FIRST call get_setup_health. Keep the full native configurator toolbox and provider/model capability handling. The generic connector's normal model preferences are qwen → llama → mistral → deepseek → gpt; resolveConfiguratorModel selects a stable canonical non-small match from the actual catalogue. Do not reorder the catalogue or preselect a different model to improve the result. Record that actual selected model and native emitted tool surface; this is a configured compatible-provider onboarding lane, not the dedicated OpenRouter credential path. | Step | Native contract |
| --- | --- |
| CSRF | GET `/api/auth/csrf-token` → `{success:true,data:{token,headerName:"X-CSRF-Token",...}}` and csrf-token Set-Cookie. Keep the actual cookie and send that returned token in X-CSRF-Token for registration. |
| Register | Same-Origin POST `/api/auth/register` `{email:"phistory@example.com",password:STRONG_PRIVATE_PASSWORD,name:"Phistory"}`. Email/password auth is enabled by default, without OAuth/SMTP setup. Native response issues accessToken in Set-Cookie and rotates CSRF. |
| Subsequent auth | Reuse the issued session token through native Cookie or **Authorization: Bearer ACTUAL_ACCESS_TOKEN**. The server explicitly supports header credentials; its CSRF checker treats explicit Bearer separately. Secure Set-Cookie on local HTTP may need a client's normal localhost cookie support or deliberate extraction of that actual issued token into its supported Bearer header; never invent/sign a token. |
| Create | POST `/api/agent` `{query:TEXT,projectType:"general"}`. The body is NDJSON despite its SSE content type; first record contains agentId/websocketUrl. Consume it through EOF so actual initialize/saveToDatabase completes. |
| Start primary task | Connect the returned `/api/agent/ID/ws` with the same real local identity and an allowed localhost Origin. Wait for native **agent_connected**, then send **{type:"generate_all"}**. This calls the real behavior.generateAllFiles and AgenticProjectBuilderOperation. It is UI/control WebSocket transport, not a model-provider WebSocket. |

Primary marker is `You are an autonomous project builder specializing in Cloudflare Workers, Durable Objects, TypeScript, React, Vite, and modern web applications.` plus the native task context. The actual operation constructs15 ToolDefinition objects in fresh scratch mode, including blueprint/filesystem/build/preview/git and native completion; count final emitted JSON tools from the real request because a definition may expand through normal conversion. Preserve every registered schema, without executing a browser/deploy tool just for capture. Stop only after this full primary request, not after initialization or an auxiliary selector. [Actual CSRF contract](https://github.com/cloudflare/vibesdk/blob/32c9e429cd2712ea5959a89f65ae434162b89229/worker/api/controllers/auth/controller.ts#L799), [normal registration and issued cookie](https://github.com/cloudflare/vibesdk/blob/32c9e429cd2712ea5959a89f65ae434162b89229/worker/api/controllers/auth/controller.ts#L50), [supported Bearer extraction](https://github.com/cloudflare/vibesdk/blob/32c9e429cd2712ea5959a89f65ae434162b89229/worker/utils/authUtils.ts#L70), [real project stream and App construction](https://github.com/cloudflare/vibesdk/blob/32c9e429cd2712ea5959a89f65ae434162b89229/worker/api/controllers/agent/controller.ts#L202), [owner-authenticated WS](https://github.com/cloudflare/vibesdk/blob/32c9e429cd2712ea5959a89f65ae434162b89229/worker/api/routes/codegenRoutes.ts#L25), [actual generate_all dispatch](https://github.com/cloudflare/vibesdk/blob/32c9e429cd2712ea5959a89f65ae434162b89229/worker/agents/core/websocket.ts#L44), [native primary system](https://github.com/cloudflare/vibesdk/blob/32c9e429cd2712ea5959a89f65ae434162b89229/worker/agents/operations/prompts/agenticBuilderPrompts.ts#L9), [full native tool construction](https://github.com/cloudflare/vibesdk/blob/32c9e429cd2712ea5959a89f65ae434162b89229/worker/agents/operations/AgenticProjectBuilder.ts#L248). Cache: `/private/tmp/phistory-batch10-contracts/vibesdk--*`. This is the named general-agentic surface, pending Linux runtime validation; it does not claim current Think or all later deployment stages.

Official release/source 1.5.0 (`32c9e429cd2712ea5959a89f65ae434162b89229`), Node >=22/Bun, documents **local-only setup** without remote Cloudflare resources. Use normal local D1/KV/R2/Durable Object state and template bootstrap, `bun run db:migrate:local`, then `bun run dev`; email registration/login works without GitHub/Google OAuth. POST `/api/agent` with `{query:TEXT}` to initialize its real coding-agent Durable Object. The endpoint itself requires normal authenticated app creation. ### Eigent native local Brain task API

Official release 1.0.8/current `eb1b6315fcc7e09305641b05e254bcdc4ef0e2c6` includes the genuine standalone Python Brain; backend Python is **>=3.11,<3.12**, with its pinned `camel-ai[eigent]`. Start its normal backend `uv run uvicorn main:api --host 127.0.0.1 --port 5001` from backend after installing complete source dependencies. POST `/chat` streams the actual workforce task. Minimal required native Chat fields are task_id, project_id, question, email, model_platform, model_type and api_key; a configured direct OpenAI model/base can use fake credentials, private project/workspace storage and normal defaults. `session_mode` defaults workforce; single-agent is a distinct supported mode. [Native server entry](https://github.com/eigent-ai/eigent/blob/eb1b6315fcc7e09305641b05e254bcdc4ef0e2c6/backend/README.md), [backend dependency contract](https://github.com/eigent-ai/eigent/blob/eb1b6315fcc7e09305641b05e254bcdc4ef0e2c6/backend/pyproject.toml), [actual Chat schema](https://github.com/eigent-ai/eigent/blob/eb1b6315fcc7e09305641b05e254bcdc4ef0e2c6/backend/app/model/chat.py#L70), [normal task controller](https://github.com/eigent-ai/eigent/blob/eb1b6315fcc7e09305641b05e254bcdc4ef0e2c6/backend/app/controller/chat_controller.py#L2075).

The control API is not anonymously usable: native local capability EIGENT_LOCAL_CONTROL_CAPABILITY is consumed at startup and presented as **X-Eigent-Local-Capability**; without that capability or a real configured Brain auth provider it fails closed. Keep the source-owned boundary. Browser preparation occurs before inference when no existing CDP descriptor is supplied and may launch a real browser, so this full task route depends on the user's browser-capture scope. Do not fabricate a CDP descriptor or remove browser tools to skip that step. The native `single_agent(Chat)` factory is another real assembled surface, but its toolkits still need real workspace/terminal/MCP/browser resources; replacing those with fake assembly would invalidate capture. [Native capability handling](https://github.com/eigent-ai/eigent/blob/eb1b6315fcc7e09305641b05e254bcdc4ef0e2c6/backend/app/auth/local_control.py), [pre-inference browser admission](https://github.com/eigent-ai/eigent/blob/eb1b6315fcc7e09305641b05e254bcdc4ef0e2c6/backend/app/controller/chat_controller.py#L1340), [actual single-agent factory](https://github.com/eigent-ai/eigent/blob/eb1b6315fcc7e09305641b05e254bcdc4ef0e2c6/backend/app/agent/factory/single_agent.py#L55).

Native startup probes the actual socai capabilities before classification, then obtains real available commands/candidate actions and runs its own decision loop. A fake socai executable or test client cannot establish this harness. Browser launches/navigation, if selected, depend on the browser scope and actual platform login state. [Official package/CLI](https://github.com/socai-io/jev-social/blob/024e96f0d8bb0ba43a9233ac30e2f803dbecee2d/package.json), [bounded native command](https://github.com/socai-io/jev-social/blob/024e96f0d8bb0ba43a9233ac30e2f803dbecee2d/bin/jev-social.js#L80), [real capability/action loop](https://github.com/socai-io/jev-social/blob/024e96f0d8bb0ba43a9233ac30e2f803dbecee2d/src/app.js#L84).

This client's primary model protocol is **not Chat Completions**: native OpenRouter calls `https://openrouter.ai/api/alpha/decisions`, model `~typesafe/jev-latest`; the alternative is a real loopback `/v1/systemone` service, native default `kev-latest`. Bodies carry structured state/questions/candidate actions; the complete remote service-owned system prompt is not transmitted by this application. Honest runtime collection can archive that decision API contract and actual candidate actions, but cannot relabel it a complete upstream LLM system prompt or invent standard function schemas. A usable capture-only decision response is needed to advance past its auxiliary platform classifier to the primary action stage; a classifier-only or final report-synthesis call is insufficient. Source extraction remains supplemental for the inaccessible service-owned prompt. [Actual endpoint and protocol](https://github.com/socai-io/jev-social/blob/024e96f0d8bb0ba43a9233ac30e2f803dbecee2d/src/decision-provider.js#L28), [native primary action stage](https://github.com/socai-io/jev-social/blob/024e96f0d8bb0ba43a9233ac30e2f803dbecee2d/src/app.js#L200).

These are source/binary-backed next-trial contracts. They do not increase the verified runtime count. Preserve the earlier Linux failure records until the main request and independent fresh comparison succeed.

### Standard Python dependency constraints for DevOpsGPT and SOC

Both failures can be retried with ordinary dependency versions inside their private Python3.10/3.11 environments. DevOpsGPT pins **openai==1.8.0** but leaves HTTPX unbounded; the SDK's own declared interval includes HTTPX0.27.2. HTTPX0.28 removed the proxies argument used by those old SDK constructors. Install normally with `uv pip install --python VENV/bin/python -r requirements.txt 'httpx==0.27.2'`. This preserves the original SDK/harness code and every native prompt/tool. It is a resolver constraint, not a monkeypatch. Keep its native CodeBasic API/model configuration above. [Original requirements](https://github.com/kuafuai/DevOpsGPT/blob/dba1cb5ec9145ac871168e7f2e2d2b35c43d109b/requirements.txt), [official OpenAI1.8 dependency metadata](https://pypi.org/pypi/openai/1.8.0/json), [HTTPX0.28 removal](https://github.com/encode/httpx/releases/tag/0.28.0).

SOC1.5.8 additionally imports undeclared pkg_resources, which ordinary **setuptools==80.9.0** still supplies. Its PyPI distribution pins **openai==1.2.3** and permits httpx>=0.25.2, but its actual **ollama0.1.6** dependency requires **httpx>=0.25.2,<0.26.0**. The earlier0.27.2 suggestion missed this transitive upper bound. Use the compatible intersection **httpx==0.25.2**: `uv pip install --python VENV/bin/python 'self-operating-computer==1.5.8' 'setuptools==80.9.0' 'httpx==0.25.2'`, retaining all normal dependencies. This older HTTPX also preserves the proxies constructor accepted by the pinned SDK. Setuptools82 removed pkg_resources; the official project directs dependent environments to older setuptools. The exact official80.9.0 wheel was downloaded without execution, its published SHA256 matched **062d34222ad13e0cc312a4c02d73f059e86a4acbfbdea8f8f76b28c99f306922**, and pkg_resources/__init__.py is present. These are standard packages, not a compatibility shim. The actual Xvfb/screenshot/default OCR-mode task remains unchanged. [SOC1.5.8 dependencies](https://pypi.org/pypi/self-operating-computer/1.5.8/json), [exact Ollama upper bound](https://pypi.org/pypi/ollama/0.1.6/json), [OpenAI1.2.3 metadata](https://pypi.org/pypi/openai/1.2.3/json), [setuptools82 boundary](https://setuptools.pypa.io/en/stable/history.html#v82-0-0), [official80.9.0 wheel](https://pypi.org/pypi/setuptools/80.9.0/json). Cache: `/private/tmp/phistory-batch8-contracts/` dependency metadata/wheel, `/private/tmp/phistory-batch9-contracts/openai-1.2.3-metadata.json` and `/private/tmp/phistory-batch11-contracts/ollama-0.1.6-metadata.json`.
