[Official two-service compose](https://github.com/ASCIT31/Dark-Moon/blob/edfe9cb12a263036a0c3dc1af604713e8197cce9/docker-compose.yml), [real embedded OpenCode/MCP image](https://github.com/ASCIT31/Dark-Moon/blob/edfe9cb12a263036a0c3dc1af604713e8197cce9/Dockerfile.opencode), [normal provider configuration](https://github.com/ASCIT31/Dark-Moon/blob/edfe9cb12a263036a0c3dc1af604713e8197cce9/docs/full.md#ii3b-opencodeenv-file).

Official Docker Hub metadata returned these **linux/amd64** published images; pin the observed digest through an ordinary private Compose overlay and archive it with the request rather than relying on mutable latest:

| Service | Image | Compressed download size |
| --- | --- | --- |
| darkmoon | ascit/darkmoon@sha256:1d17e1d148f4e4f90f24a6904dd4d4117e8b845c573ebb7d2b00585cebe60326 | 6,666,987,968 bytes |
| opencode | ascit/opencode-darkmoon@sha256:3ab36bc62953fb1ea5e1e8ee9c4ce78ee46faa3735932f9ce60b0c28385f713b | 359,310,716 bytes |

This is roughly7.0GB of compressed transfer plus extracted layers and Docker working space. Use a dedicated large-disk job;30–40GB free is a planning allowance, not a measured upstream minimum. The expensive model-serving hardware tables in the documentation do not apply when the provider is an owned capture-only endpoint. Published opencode image last_updated is2026-10-27T19:18:24Z, four minutes after current Dark-Moon HEAD's commit time; that timing is only a provenance clue. Verify actual installed image prompt/plugin/server files against source before describing it as the current source surface. Keep actual image digests, host binary version/hash and installed Python requirements in metadata. [Official toolbox tag metadata](https://hub.docker.com/v2/repositories/ascit/darkmoon/tags/latest), [official OpenCode tag metadata](https://hub.docker.com/v2/repositories/ascit/opencode-darkmoon/tags/latest), [current exact commit](https://github.com/ASCIT31/Dark-Moon/commit/edfe9cb12a263036a0c3dc1af604713e8197cce9).

**Normal configuration and launch.** In an isolated complete source copy, create the ordinary **.opencode.env** before startup. Its contents can be:

```dotenv
OPENCODE_LOCAL_MODE=true
OPENCODE_LOCAL_PROVIDER_ID=openai
OPENCODE_LOCAL_PROVIDER_NAME=Local OpenAI-compatible endpoint
OPENCODE_LOCAL_BASE_URL=http://host.docker.internal:TAP_PORT/v1
OPENCODE_LOCAL_MODEL=gpt-4.1
OPENCODE_LOCAL_API_KEY=phistory-fake-api-key
```

These are real supported operator inputs, not a generated replacement prompt. apply-settings.sh writes the native @ai-sdk/openai-compatible provider with baseURL/apiKey, both model/small_model and all50 role entries; it also retains the real MCP and both plugin registrations. Using recognized openai/gpt-4.1 allows the actual fork to inherit native model metadata rather than inventing a model catalogue. This is explicitly a **configured local-compatible pentest role**, not a universal DarkMoon default. Its no-provider fallback opencode/big-pickle is refused by the real headless launcher. Source supports separate cloud/on-prem choices; no real provider key is needed for this compatible capture lane. [Native env-to-config conversion](https://github.com/ASCIT31/Dark-Moon/blob/edfe9cb12a263036a0c3dc1af604713e8197cce9/conf/apply-settings.sh#L36), [real provider schema/metadata merge](https://github.com/ASCIT31/opencode/blob/77b3b46788623317115ae920cf0072e54aa2643c/packages/opencode/src/provider/provider.ts#L965), [actual headless free-tier refusal](https://github.com/ASCIT31/Dark-Moon/blob/edfe9cb12a263036a0c3dc1af604713e8197cce9/darkmoon.sh#L120).

A standard Compose overlay for the capture environment adds **opencode.extra_hosts=["host.docker.internal:host-gateway"]** and the two digest-pinned image values. Bind the owned endpoint on a host interface reachable through that Docker gateway; host127.0.0.1 is not reachable as container127.0.0.1. HTTP operator baseURL avoids TLS interception/CA differences while leaving the native prompt/tools intact. For a named transparent HTTPS alternative, use ordinary container HTTP_PROXY/HTTPS_PROXY pointing at the gateway, NO_PROXY=127.0.0.1,localhost to preserve local MCP, and a read-only mounted capture CA with **NODE_EXTRA_CA_CERTS** pointing to its container path. Bun documents proxy environment support and CA-bundle loading; exact published-runtime transport behavior still needs the Linux trial. Do not disable TLS verification. Preserve all original compose mounts, entrypoints, capability settings and native MCP transport. [Docker extra_hosts](https://docs.docker.com/reference/compose-file/services/#extra_hosts), [Bun proxy/NO_PROXY](https://bun.com/docs/runtime/networking/fetch#proxying-requests), [Bun CA-bundle support](https://bun.com/blog/bun-v1.2.3#load-full-certificate-bundles-from-node_extra_ca_certs).

Normal amd64 **./install.sh** skips its interactive form when this .opencode.env is already complete, then starts the official image-based stack. Its purported --provider/--model examples in another script are not actual install.sh options: current parser only handles --init/--reset/help. It also runs project down/purges and a **global docker builder prune -f**. On a shared capture runner use the equivalent documented native Compose operations in a fresh owned source/state directory: create its bind directories, `docker compose -f docker-compose.yml -f PRIVATE_OVERLAY pull`, then `... up -d`. Entrypoints perform the real configuration, role/workflow seeding and MCP bootstrap; no installer-owned application initialization is omitted. Pre-pull the large public images before the intercepted task. Tear down only this owned stack afterward. [Actual installer options and operations](https://github.com/ASCIT31/Dark-Moon/blob/edfe9cb12a263036a0c3dc1af604713e8197cce9/install.sh#L23), [native configuration/seeding](https://github.com/ASCIT31/Dark-Moon/blob/edfe9cb12a263036a0c3dc1af604713e8197cce9/conf/entrypoint-opencode.sh#L35).

For the distinct official source-build channel, build the unmodified **Dockerfile** and **Dockerfile.opencode**, then select those real images through ordinary Compose image/pull_policy configuration. Dockerfile.opencode uses Bun1.3.11 and clones ASCIT31/opencode; current fork HEAD **77b3b46788623317115ae920cf0072e54aa2643c** actually declares bun@1.3.11 and package1.3.0, matching the Dockerfile despite its stale1.3.14 comment. The fork clone is mutable, so retain the actual builder's Git SHA/binary provenance rather than claiming that Dark-Moon HEAD fixes it. The official docker-compose-dev.yml also declares gpus:all; do not use that unchanged launch file on a CPU-only runner and then mislabel its hardware refusal as a required first-request GPU. The normal published amd64 compose has no GPU requirement. [Official source Dockerfiles](https://github.com/ASCIT31/Dark-Moon/blob/edfe9cb12a263036a0c3dc1af604713e8197cce9/Dockerfile.opencode#L1), [actual fork package manager](https://github.com/ASCIT31/opencode/blob/77b3b46788623317115ae920cf0072e54aa2643c/package.json#L7), [dev-compose GPU declaration](https://github.com/ASCIT31/Dark-Moon/blob/edfe9cb12a263036a0c3dc1af604713e8197cce9/docker-compose-dev.yml#L23).

**Readiness and primary validation.** Native opencode startup applies config, seeds agents/workflows and starts the persistent **http://127.0.0.1:8181/mcp** server in that same container. Privacy's actual **/tmp/darkmoon-privacy.sock** is mode0600 and shares the same in-process vault. Wait with an overall deadline for that socket and the native **opencode mcp list** connected result; preserve `/tmp/darkmoon-mcp-boot.log`. Do not replace the tokenizer or set its fail-open/disable controls. Its real plugin retries startup and otherwise replaces the task with a prompt-withheld marker, which must fail primary task validation. It runs both chat.message and experimental.chat.messages.transform so initial/title/main requests retain the native privacy boundary. apply-settings also starts its ordinary background TUI warmup; this supplies no task and must not be mistaken for the requested primary run. [Persistent MCP and socket boot](https://github.com/ASCIT31/Dark-Moon/blob/edfe9cb12a263036a0c3dc1af604713e8197cce9/conf/entrypoint-opencode.sh#L155), [actual tokenization protocol](https://github.com/ASCIT31/Dark-Moon/blob/edfe9cb12a263036a0c3dc1af604713e8197cce9/mcp/src/prompt_socket.py), [full native hooks/fail-closed behavior](https://github.com/ASCIT31/Dark-Moon/blob/edfe9cb12a263036a0c3dc1af604713e8197cce9/conf/plugins/darkmoon-privacy.ts), [warmup behavior](https://github.com/ASCIT31/Dark-Moon/blob/edfe9cb12a263036a0c3dc1af604713e8197cce9/conf/apply-settings.sh#L538).

Use the actual headless wrapper with a benign task first and role afterward: **./darkmoon.sh 'Reply with one short sentence.' --agent pentest --format json**, non-TTY stdin. Keep all native role/tool permissions. Its complete seeded pentest source is120871bytes and contains **STATUS QUALIFICATION — DARKMOON** and **You are a Strategic Vulnerability Assessment Orchestrator**; require a native role marker and the real task in the main request. At current fork source, absence of explicit agent falls back to build, while --agent is validated and can fall back on an invalid/subagent role; inspect logs and reject such fallback. Never substitute a bare host run. Native tool registration contains **13** MCP functions, despite the stale startup banner saying11: get_session, tokenize_prompt, health_check, check_tool, diagnose, execute_command, list_allowed_tools, list_workflows, run_workflow, dashboard_init_campaign, dashboard_push_finding, dashboard_push_infra_node, dashboard_finalize_campaign. The real fork converts these to **darkmoon_NAME** model tools. Require the actual native schemas and preserve OpenCode's additional tools; do not infer completeness from the banner or from one builtin tool. [Native task dispatch](https://github.com/ASCIT31/Dark-Moon/blob/edfe9cb12a263036a0c3dc1af604713e8197cce9/darkmoon.sh#L149), [full primary role](https://github.com/ASCIT31/Dark-Moon/blob/edfe9cb12a263036a0c3dc1af604713e8197cce9/conf/agents/pentest.md), [actual fork default agent](https://github.com/ASCIT31/opencode/blob/77b3b46788623317115ae920cf0072e54aa2643c/packages/opencode/src/agent/agent.ts#L274), [real MCP registration](https://github.com/ASCIT31/Dark-Moon/blob/edfe9cb12a263036a0c3dc1af604713e8197cce9/mcp/src/server.py), [real tool-name conversion](https://github.com/ASCIT31/opencode/blob/77b3b46788623317115ae920cf0072e54aa2643c/packages/opencode/src/mcp/index.ts#L606).

**No automatic browser launch before this first task:** toolbox entrypoint probes GPU availability then execs its original sleep-infinity CMD. MCP registration discovers workflow classes; their constructors only retain state. Startup health checks Docker status, command-v tool availability and df; it does not call diagnose/network scans or a browser. Chromium/Playwright/Lightpanda are real installed capabilities, whose execution remains later tool activity. Preserve them while collecting the no-tool-call first request. [Actual toolbox entry/CMD](https://github.com/ASCIT31/Dark-Moon/blob/edfe9cb12a263036a0c3dc1af604713e8197cce9/Dockerfile#L374), [GPU-only startup](https://github.com/ASCIT31/Dark-Moon/blob/edfe9cb12a263036a0c3dc1af604713e8197cce9/conf/entrypoint-darkmoon.sh), [startup health probe](https://github.com/ASCIT31/Dark-Moon/blob/edfe9cb12a263036a0c3dc1af604713e8197cce9/mcp/src/tools/core/health.py#L47), [workflow registration](https://github.com/ASCIT31/Dark-Moon/blob/edfe9cb12a263036a0c3dc1af604713e8197cce9/mcp/src/tools/workflows/list_workflows.py#L45), [ordinary constructor](https://github.com/ASCIT31/Dark-Moon/blob/edfe9cb12a263036a0c3dc1af604713e8197cce9/mcp/src/tools/workflows/base.py#L44).

Useful current-source fingerprints for checking the published image: pentest.md **0beefa473d8fc5f05e53ccd4c0371e460fb297232ddde33aecf96d30fd48d7de**; darkmoon-privacy.ts **d1d6149245fa8c96bfe3bfc8f794a14ea3206364ebbc7a934216cfd0bcbb0bb2**; mcp/src/server.py **5074fad425e0a42459ede9113982f5bceb98d32cc159a203fd872803e21be818**. Preserve a mismatch as actual packaging/version evidence, not a successful current-source match. All full source, fork contracts, Docker Hub metadata and hashes are cached under **/private/tmp/phistory-batch11-contracts/**; original source paths remain under `/private/tmp/phistory-batch8-contracts/dark-moon-*`. No local images, real CLI or model were run during this audit. Runtime/fresh status remains pending.

## Non-browser integration cost and primary validation markers

This cost ordering concerns genuine native primary requests on CPU Linux. Critic-training, public SoulForge and AutoRef have since passed independent validation; DevOpsGPT currently fails its legacy OpenAI/httpx compatibility, WeChat awaits fresh validation, and DB-GPT remains a concrete next candidate. Dependency floors below come from each pinned source; a lockfile, when present, remains the exact installation authority. Zero JSON tools is valid for a native textual action protocol or prompt-only task. Validate a project-specific primary marker and task input rather than counting the first intercepted API call.

| Priority | Native lane and exact dependency surface | Primary request marker and scope |
|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | **critic-training initial actor**: Python >=3.10; `python -m pip install -e mini-swe-agent` from the pinned fork. Its core dependencies are PyYAML, requests, Jinja2, LiteLLM >=1.75.5, tenacity, rich, python-dotenv, typer, platformdirs, textual, prompt_toolkit and OpenAI excluding 1.100.0/1.100.1. No full/dev extra, GPU, vLLM or datasets is required for this initial actor. | System contains `You are a helpful assistant that can interact with a computer.` and the one-bash-block rule; native task in user message. Preserve default.yaml and real LocalEnvironment; JSON tools may be zero. This is not evidence the trained critic stage ran. [Fork manifest](https://github.com/shubhamrgandhi/critic-training/blob/1c4aa0d3c28c522fe9e7541b14b86fe0c4a13e5c/mini-swe-agent/pyproject.toml), [actor prompt](https://github.com/shubhamrgandhi/critic-training/blob/1c4aa0d3c28c522fe9e7541b14b86fe0c4a13e5c/mini-swe-agent/src/minisweagent/config/default.yaml). |
| 2 | **public Empryo/SoulForge v2**: Bun >=1.3.13, `bun install --frozen-lockfile`, retain Linux x64/arm64 OpenTUI optional runtime packages. Full lock includes AI SDK6, OpenAI provider3, real MCP/tree-sitter/terminal/index libraries; no X display, GPU or Electron is needed for native headless execution. | Configured OpenAI-family main system contains `<persistence>` and `<tool_preambles>`, with actual native tool schemas and task TEXT. Native Soul Map/project context is separate user context; an indexing/map-only helper is not the main task. Keep native headless max-steps1 bound. ## Refined contracts for the next host/API drivers

**Markus:** set `server.apiPort=PORT` and `server.webPort=PORT+1` in the private normal config, then `markus --config PRIVATE_CONFIG start`. Current CLI declares no port flag; the later globalOpts.port read does not make `--port` valid. Native login issues **Set-Cookie markus_token**, and returns `{user:{id,name,email,role,orgId,...},needsOnboarding:true}` rather than a top-level token. Reuse an ordinary cookie jar for GET `/api/agents` and POST `/api/agents/ID/message` `{text:TEXT}`. Cache: `/private/tmp/phistory-batch9-contracts/markus--packages__cli__src__index.ts`, `markus--packages__cli__src__commands__start.ts`, and `markus--packages__org-manager__src__api-server.ts`. [Declared CLI flags](https://github.com/markus-global/markus/blob/91f7b21c90987863dc296fedec8ff78e4f98ef20/packages/cli/src/index.ts#L26), [cookie](https://github.com/markus-global/markus/blob/91f7b21c90987863dc296fedec8ff78e4f98ef20/packages/org-manager/src/api-server.ts#L2377), [native login response](https://github.com/markus-global/markus/blob/91f7b21c90987863dc296fedec8ff78e4f98ef20/packages/org-manager/src/api-server.ts#L3359).

**LongHorizon:** install real `@openai/codex` separately through the native host installation channel, install lh-harness0.1.8, then `lh-harness init` in the actual owned Git workspace. Init is noninteractive and creates `.lh-harness/config.toml`. Run `lh-harness run --task TEXT --agent codex --max-rounds 1 --api-key FAKE --base-url https://api.openai.com/v1`. No GUI plugin is enabled by default; no plugin bootstrap is needed for this text task. The initial manager's **user** prompt has fixed marker `You are the LongHorizon-Harness manager agent.`, plus `Original task:`/TEXT and `Current management round: 1`. The real Codex system/tools are preserved. Cache: `/private/tmp/phistory-batch9-contracts/longhorizon-harness--src__lh_harness__prompt_texts.py`, role_prompts.py and manager.py. [Initial native manager prompt](https://github.com/AMAP-ML/LongHorizon-Harness/blob/634030370a9d41ac1c1c6ea7c07bc96ca1d24462/src/lh_harness/role_prompts.py#L43), [fixed manager identity](https://github.com/AMAP-ML/LongHorizon-Harness/blob/634030370a9d41ac1c1c6ea7c07bc96ca1d24462/src/lh_harness/prompt_texts.py#L68).

**Agentlas OS:** use the actual remote-release installer with `HEPHAESTUS_REF=os-v1.2.99` and HEPHAESTUS_SOURCE_DIR unset, then install the real Claude host separately through its native channel. `agentlas-os host status claude` and `agentlas-os host session claude -- -p '/agentlas-os-core:agentlas TEXT' --output-format json` use its generated immutable standalone core plugin. Do not call `host install claude`, which is not its Claude setup contract. Require the activated skill's **user body** marker `# Agentlas Core & Unified Command System` plus `Single entry point for every Agentlas subsystem and capability.` to distinguish a genuine Agentlas activation from a bare Claude request or unrecognized slash literal. `host_core._payloads` generates `skills/agentlas/SKILL.md` from this exact native skill, rewriting only generation-bound runtime paths. The standalone command catalog separately generates hep-search/call/goal; the legacy plugin's commands/agentlas.md is not this activation's body. Build compiler with `coax/build.sh`; compile a task-owned native API driver using `coax/bin/coax c DRIVER.coax > PRIVATE/driver.c`, then `cc -std=c11 -O1 -g -o PRIVATE/driver PRIVATE/driver.c coax/platform/process-native.c -lm`. ## Complete source-derived DB-GPT and UFO Linux trial contracts

### DB-GPT source packages, real Chroma and official task

Use the full official source at `584a71b36330258d42a337dbfc1d7737e16ca58a` and Python3.10/3.11. The root `dbgpt-mono` is a workspace placeholder with no runtime dependencies; installing only that root is insufficient. A focused real SDK installation from its root is:

```sh
uv venv --python 3.11 PRIVATE_VENV
uv pip install --python PRIVATE_VENV/bin/python -e 'packages/dbgpt-core[agent,client,simple_framework,framework,proxy_openai]' -e 'packages/dbgpt-ext[storage_chromadb]'
```

The official broader locked application closure is `uv sync --frozen --package dbgpt-app --no-dev`. This is a normal workspace fallback, not a requirement to start the application server. The focused command retains the actual framework, AutoLLMClient/OpenAI proxy, native resources/memory and PersistentChroma implementation. Declared constraints include SQLAlchemy>=2.0.25,<2.0.29, NumPy<2, OpenAI>=1.59.6, tiktoken>=0.8, onnxruntime>=1.14.1,<=1.18.1 and chromadb>=0.4.22. No HF/accelerator extra or GPU is needed for the native remote-embedding example; dbgpt-acc-auto itself has no mandatory dependencies, while torch/CUDA are optional extras. Source inspection established this dependency contract; Linux import/request validation is still required. [Workspace definition](https://github.com/eosphoros-ai/DB-GPT/blob/584a71b36330258d42a337dbfc1d7737e16ca58a/pyproject.toml), [core groups](https://github.com/eosphoros-ai/DB-GPT/blob/584a71b36330258d42a337dbfc1d7737e16ca58a/packages/dbgpt-core/pyproject.toml), [real Chroma extra](https://github.com/eosphoros-ai/DB-GPT/blob/584a71b36330258d42a337dbfc1d7737e16ca58a/packages/dbgpt-ext/pyproject.toml#L77), [optional accelerator dependencies](https://github.com/eosphoros-ai/DB-GPT/blob/584a71b36330258d42a337dbfc1d7737e16ca58a/packages/dbgpt-accelerator/dbgpt-acc-auto/pyproject.toml).

Inside tap, from an owned isolated source/workspace and private HOME, run `PRIVATE_VENV/bin/python examples/agents/react_agent_example.py` with fake SILICONFLOW_API_KEY and ordinary native model selection left at proxy/siliconflow / Qwen/Qwen2.5-Coder-32B-Instruct. This is the project's own ReAct task, not a caller-rebuilt prompt. Preserve the three native actions, HybridMemory, real persistent Chroma state and BAAI/bge-large-zh-v1.5 remote embedding route. Public asset/dependency downloads may be prepared outside tap, but **actual remote embedding/model API calls may not**. If an embedding request precedes the main call, archive it as auxiliary and provide a normal capture-only embedding response to let the native memory path continue; do not remove memory or replace its store. Main request validation requires the native ReActToolMaster/ACTION SPACE prompt and actual example task. AgentContext's default native-function-calling flag is false, so JSON-tools0 is valid and must not cause a mode change. [Official complete task example](https://github.com/eosphoros-ai/DB-GPT/blob/584a71b36330258d42a337dbfc1d7737e16ca58a/examples/agents/react_agent_example.py), [actual persistent store](https://github.com/eosphoros-ai/DB-GPT/blob/584a71b36330258d42a337dbfc1d7737e16ca58a/packages/dbgpt-ext/src/dbgpt_ext/storage/vector_store/chroma_store.py#L87), [native default action mode](https://github.com/eosphoros-ai/DB-GPT/blob/584a71b36330258d42a337dbfc1d7737e16ca58a/packages/dbgpt-core/src/dbgpt/agent/core/agent.py#L235).

Cached source is under `/private/tmp/phistory-batch7-contracts/db-gpt--*`, including the root/core/ext/app/accelerator pyproject files, the real ReAct example, agent.py, react_agent.py, Chroma store, embedding modules and long-term memory implementation. This is an ordinary CPU SDK candidate; the initial capture does not require a new large database service, browser or special hardware.

### UFO nonvisual Linux DeviceAgent, authenticated local services

Use complete source `eae3c0a8d168c47cb10df0652e293e092eda1078`, Python3.10 and its native requirements in a private source checkout/HOME. Copy `config/ufo/agents.yaml.template` to agents.yaml and replace placeholders with fake keys, preserving APP_AGENT's OpenAI/gpt-4o Chat Completions defaults and native Linux prompt/MCP configuration. **No X display, screenshot or desktop session is required before the main LinuxAgent request.** LinuxAgent passes is_visual=None, its get_prompter explicitly disables visual mode, and LinuxLLMInteractionStrategy builds request/plan/blackboard/last-actions text directly. LinuxBaseSession creates this actual registered agent without Windows HostAgent. [Real Linux agent construction](https://github.com/microsoft/UFO/blob/eae3c0a8d168c47cb10df0652e293e092eda1078/ufo/agents/agent/customized_agent.py#L46), [nonvisual primary strategy](https://github.com/microsoft/UFO/blob/eae3c0a8d168c47cb10df0652e293e092eda1078/ufo/agents/processors/strategies/linux_agent_strategy.py#L64), [native Linux session](https://github.com/microsoft/UFO/blob/eae3c0a8d168c47cb10df0652e293e092eda1078/ufo/module/sessions/platform_session.py#L49).

Start three real isolated local components; the current source has control authentication absent from the older quickstart command snippets:

```sh
UFO_MCP_API_KEY=PRIVATE_MCP_CONTROL python -m ufo.client.mcp.http_servers.linux_mcp_server --host localhost --port 8010
python -m ufo.server.app --host 127.0.0.1 --port 5001 --platform linux --api-key PRIVATE_SERVER_CONTROL
python -m ufo.client.client --ws --ws-server 'ws://127.0.0.1:5001/ws?token=PRIVATE_SERVER_CONTROL' --client-id phistory_linux --platform linux --request TEXT --task_name phistory
```

These are ordinary subprocess/service starts, not three sequential blocking commands in one shell. The two PRIVATE control values are isolated, task-owned local service credentials; they are separate from the fake model key. The MCP server exits if UFO_MCP_API_KEY is absent. The device server verifies its WebSocket query token; omitting it fails authentication. Its native Linux MCP configuration contains only an action endpoint `BashExecutor/http/localhost:8010/mcp`, without a data_collection screenshot server. The native client waits for its connected_event, then starts the supplied task; it intentionally remains alive afterwards, so use the overall capture wall deadline and process cleanup. A port-ready/MCP initialization check is appropriate before the client starts; provider capture must already cover the actual server/agent process. [MCP credential requirement and CLI](https://github.com/microsoft/UFO/blob/eae3c0a8d168c47cb10df0652e293e092eda1078/ufo/client/mcp/http_servers/linux_mcp_server.py#L861), [device-server authentication](https://github.com/microsoft/UFO/blob/eae3c0a8d168c47cb10df0652e293e092eda1078/ufo/server/app.py#L75), [native client task start](https://github.com/microsoft/UFO/blob/eae3c0a8d168c47cb10df0652e293e092eda1078/ufo/client/client.py#L100), [actual Linux MCP endpoint](https://github.com/microsoft/UFO/blob/eae3c0a8d168c47cb10df0652e293e092eda1078/config/ufo/mcp.yaml#L139).

Primary system marker is `You are **LinuxAgent**, the UFO framework's intelligent agent`; the actual native prompt says to operate through Linux bash/APIs and not reference screenshots/GUI components. Preserve its native JSON/text action protocol; a positive OpenAI function-tool count is not assumed. Cached exact source under `/private/tmp/phistory-batch10-contracts/ufo--*` includes the Linux prompt, customized agent, strategy, platform/Linux sessions, client/server entrypoints, MCP server and three native config files. This is a supported Linux task surface with real local services, not a substitute Windows UI trace. [Native primary prompt](https://github.com/microsoft/UFO/blob/eae3c0a8d168c47cb10df0652e293e092eda1078/ufo/prompts/third_party/linux_agent.yaml#L3).

### LoopTroop 0.6.4: native foreground daemon and initial relevant-files task

The public `looptroop` npm package is 0.6.4, has bin `dist/server/cli/launcher.cjs`, and requires **Node>=24.19.0**. The inspected current source is `ec6cb77d52898bbcee838ce7139a849a3fcb5f09`, also version0.6.4; npm metadata has no gitHead, so record the actual npm integrity separately from the inspected source commit. Install the real OpenCode host through its normal package channel and put its executable on PATH. Do not replace it with an adapter fixture or mock mode. A bounded no-browser start is:

```sh
LOOPTROOP_CONFIG_DIR=PRIVATE_CONFIG LOOPTROOP_OPENCODE_MODE=live LOOPTROOP_OPENCODE_BASE_URL=http://127.0.0.1:OPENCODE_PORT looptroop start --foreground --port DAEMON_PORT
```

The two port placeholders must be expanded to distinct actual integers. The real supervisor finds `opencode` on PATH and starts `opencode serve --hostname BIND_HOST --port OPENCODE_PORT`; it normally generates private OpenCode server authentication itself. Provider fake credentials and the ordinary isolated OpenCode model configuration are inherited. If adopting an already running real OpenCode server, its matching OPENCODE_PASSWORD/OPENCODE_SERVER_PASSWORD must be supplied; letting the supervisor own a new private service is simpler. There is no universal LoopTroop model default: select a real model exposed by this OpenCode instance and name the capture as that configured lane. [Published metadata](https://registry.npmjs.org/looptroop/latest), [CLI start](https://github.com/looptroop-ai/LoopTroop/blob/ec6cb77d52898bbcee838ce7139a849a3fcb5f09/server/cli/cli.ts#L85), [real supervisor](https://github.com/looptroop-ai/LoopTroop/blob/ec6cb77d52898bbcee838ce7139a849a3fcb5f09/server/opencode/supervisor.ts), [native runtime configuration](https://github.com/looptroop-ai/LoopTroop/blob/ec6cb77d52898bbcee838ce7139a849a3fcb5f09/server/opencode/runtimeConfig.ts).

The native owner-only state is **PRIVATE_CONFIG/daemon.json**, not an assumed stdout READY line. It contains host, port, pid, instanceId, **apiToken** and opencode `{baseUrl,owned,status,...}`. Wait with an overall deadline for this actual live daemon's state, managed/adopted rather than degraded OpenCode, authenticated GET `/api/health` `{status:"ok",...}`, and GET `/api/health/opencode` `{status:"ok",models:[...],protocol?,version,...}`. Read the local apiToken internally and send `Authorization: Bearer TOKEN` or x-looptroop-token. Do not serialize this control secret into trace metadata/logs. GET `/api/health/startup` is additional restoration status, not a substitute for OpenCode readiness. [State shape/path](https://github.com/looptroop-ai/LoopTroop/blob/ec6cb77d52898bbcee838ce7139a849a3fcb5f09/server/lib/daemonPaths.ts), [state written after real runtime startup](https://github.com/looptroop-ai/LoopTroop/blob/ec6cb77d52898bbcee838ce7139a849a3fcb5f09/server/daemon/startDaemon.ts#L420), [health response contracts](https://github.com/looptroop-ai/LoopTroop/blob/ec6cb77d52898bbcee838ce7139a849a3fcb5f09/server/routes/health.ts), [native token headers](https://github.com/looptroop-ai/LoopTroop/blob/ec6cb77d52898bbcee838ce7139a849a3fcb5f09/server/middleware/authUtils.ts).

Submit an ordinary project/ticket through these authenticated routes:

| Step | Exact request and response field |
| --- | --- |
| Profile | GET `/api/profile`; POST `/api/profile` when null, otherwise PATCH it. Body `{mainImplementer:MODEL_A,councilMembers:JSON.stringify([MODEL_A,MODEL_B]),minCouncilQuorum:2}`. **councilMembers is a JSON string**, not an array. **At least two distinct model IDs including the main implementer are mandatory**; the former single-model recipe receives400. Use actual catalogue entries such as openai/gpt-4.1 and openai/gpt-4.1-mini, retaining native quorum2/default. Model validation uses the real OpenCode model catalogue. Keep native interview budgets, retries and tool policies. The returned profile is the row itself, with id. |
| Project | POST `/api/projects` `{name:"Phistory",shortname:"PHY",folderPath:ABS_REAL_GITHUB_CHECKOUT,profileId:PROFILE_ID}`. shortname is 3–5 uppercase alphanumeric characters; profileId is optional. The returned project row has id. |
| Ticket | POST `/api/tickets` `{projectId:PROJECT_ID,title:TEXT}`. The returned ticket itself has **id**; preserve it verbatim and URL-encode it as a path component. Do not assume it is an integer. |
| Task start | POST `/api/tickets/TICKET_ID/start`, no JSON body required. Only a genuine DRAFT ticket can start. Response confirms accepted workflow state; the actor emits the request asynchronously. Keep capture active until the primary request is complete, then clean up only owned processes. |

**The project must be an actual Git repository whose origin resolves to github.com.** An empty directory, plain git-init directory or non-GitHub origin gets400. Use an owned disposable checkout of a real public GitHub project, rather than fabricating a remote URL. Normal ticket initialization performs a bounded `git fetch --no-progress --prune origin` and creates its native local branch/worktree before the first task request; this path does not push a commit before that first prompt. Clone/public Git metadata can be prepared outside tap, while model requests remain capture-only. [Profile schema](https://github.com/looptroop-ai/LoopTroop/blob/ec6cb77d52898bbcee838ce7139a849a3fcb5f09/server/routes/profiles.ts#L98), [real GitHub-origin gate](https://github.com/looptroop-ai/LoopTroop/blob/ec6cb77d52898bbcee838ce7139a849a3fcb5f09/server/routes/projects.ts#L339), [native ticket schema](https://github.com/looptroop-ai/LoopTroop/blob/ec6cb77d52898bbcee838ce7139a849a3fcb5f09/server/routes/ticketHandlers/schemas.ts#L21), [task start](https://github.com/looptroop-ai/LoopTroop/blob/ec6cb77d52898bbcee838ce7139a849a3fcb5f09/server/routes/ticketHandlers/lifecycleHandlers.ts#L125), [real initialization](https://github.com/looptroop-ai/LoopTroop/blob/ec6cb77d52898bbcee838ce7139a849a3fcb5f09/server/ticket/initialize.ts#L352), [bounded origin fetch](https://github.com/looptroop-ai/LoopTroop/blob/ec6cb77d52898bbcee838ce7139a849a3fcb5f09/server/git/repository.ts#L92).

**Corrected after the real Linux task:** START first enters **SCANNING_RELEVANT_FILES/PROM0**, not PROM1. Its native outer-role marker is in the **user** prompt: `You are an expert software architect performing codebase analysis for implementation planning.`, with ticket title/text and the full relevant-files task. The real handler builds PROM0, uses its **read_only** policy and dispatches through the actual managed OpenCode service. The diagnostic primary request includes glob/grep/question/read schemas. This is genuine first-stage workflow work, separate from OpenCode titles/model probes. It is a concrete honest **native-relevant-files-scan** surface with full emitted host system, outer user prompt and actual read-only tools; it must not be labeled a captured interview. Its requested XML-wrapped YAML is a text output contract, not a native JSON response_format/tool_choice schema. A plain capture dummy reaches this request but fails native parsing afterward. Do not fabricate a matching RELEVANT_FILES_RESULT body to advance the workflow. [Mandatory initial transition](https://github.com/looptroop-ai/LoopTroop/blob/ec6cb77d52898bbcee838ce7139a849a3fcb5f09/server/machines/ticketMachine.ts#L185), [actual PROM0 role/policy](https://github.com/looptroop-ai/LoopTroop/blob/ec6cb77d52898bbcee838ce7139a849a3fcb5f09/server/prompts/index.ts#L159), [real dispatch and output validation](https://github.com/looptroop-ai/LoopTroop/blob/ec6cb77d52898bbcee838ce7139a849a3fcb5f09/server/workflow/phases/verificationPhase.ts#L3777).

There is **no established ordinary skip-preflight/direct-interview route** in this0.6.4 contract. SCANNING_RELEVANT_FILES accepts RELEVANT_FILES_READY only after the native parser has accepted a result and persisted its real artifact. The handler does not skip a fresh scan for an empty repository or a preexisting file. POST /tickets/ID/skip only works in WAITING_INTERVIEW_ANSWERS with the existing actual batch; PUT /interview only edits a document from WAITING_INTERVIEW_APPROVAL onward; PATCH status is explicitly403. The discovered direct skip is in **mockLifecycleHandlers**; the separate dev-event route requires a dedicated enable flag/token and injects state-machine events. Neither establishes the normal live task path and neither should be used to manufacture stage completion. Once a real scan eventually succeeds, PROM1 council interview uses the later marker `You are an expert product manager and technical interviewer.` and its own native read_only tools; that is a separate future capture. [State-machine boundary](https://github.com/looptroop-ai/LoopTroop/blob/ec6cb77d52898bbcee838ce7139a849a3fcb5f09/server/machines/ticketMachine.ts#L229), [skip state/batch gate](https://github.com/looptroop-ai/LoopTroop/blob/ec6cb77d52898bbcee838ce7139a849a3fcb5f09/server/routes/ticketHandlers/interviewHandlers.ts#L283), [interview edit state gate](https://github.com/looptroop-ai/LoopTroop/blob/ec6cb77d52898bbcee838ce7139a849a3fcb5f09/server/routes/ticketHandlers/interviewHandlers.ts#L958), [protected status](https://github.com/looptroop-ai/LoopTroop/blob/ec6cb77d52898bbcee838ce7139a849a3fcb5f09/server/routes/ticketHandlers/crudHandlers.ts#L83), [mock-only skip](https://github.com/looptroop-ai/LoopTroop/blob/ec6cb77d52898bbcee838ce7139a849a3fcb5f09/server/workflow/runner.ts#L120), [developer-only injection](https://github.com/looptroop-ai/LoopTroop/blob/ec6cb77d52898bbcee838ce7139a849a3fcb5f09/server/routes/ticketHandlers/devEventHandlers.ts#L8), [later genuine PROM1](https://github.com/looptroop-ai/LoopTroop/blob/ec6cb77d52898bbcee838ce7139a849a3fcb5f09/server/phases/interview/deliberate.ts#L48).

Exact source caches: `/private/tmp/phistory-batch8-contracts/looptroop--*`, including supervisor, runtimeConfig, childEnvironment, daemonPaths/startDaemon, profile/projects/ticket handlers, PROM1 and deliberate.ts. Managed OpenCode retains proxy/fake-provider configuration while removing unrelated LoopTroop control credentials from project/tool children.

### AnythingLLM 1.19.0: production server, collector and REST agent task

Use full source `eb498cf2c2111b0c651d4c3781f0cdfb8168935c` (server/collector1.19.0), Node>=18.12.1 and the official Yarn1.22.19 channel. A REST-only trial needs the real **server and collector** dependency closures; frontend build is unnecessary for these routes. Install each folder's own lockfile with `yarn install --frozen-lockfile --production --network-timeout 100000`. Server's production dependencies include Prisma5.3.1 and real LanceDB; collector includes Puppeteer~21.5.2. Preserve native install scripts and Linux shared libraries. Collector's public browser download may be prepared outside tap, without launching a browser. [Native Docker installation](https://github.com/Mintplex-Labs/anything-llm/blob/eb498cf2c2111b0c651d4c3781f0cdfb8168935c/docker/Dockerfile), [server dependencies](https://github.com/Mintplex-Labs/anything-llm/blob/eb498cf2c2111b0c651d4c3781f0cdfb8168935c/server/package.json), [collector dependencies](https://github.com/Mintplex-Labs/anything-llm/blob/eb498cf2c2111b0c651d4c3781f0cdfb8168935c/collector/package.json).

The Prisma SQLite URL is **hardcoded `file:../storage/anythingllm.db` relative to server/prisma**, so DATABASE_URL alone does not relocate it. Use an actual private runtime source tree and **STORAGE_DIR=ABS_RUNTIME_SOURCE/server/storage**; keep Prisma's native DB and all other persistent state in that same private tree. Run from server cwd:

```sh
./node_modules/.bin/prisma generate --schema=./prisma/schema.prisma
./node_modules/.bin/prisma migrate deploy --schema=./prisma/schema.prisma
```

This matches production Docker startup; a fabricated schema/database or development migrate-dev is unnecessary. Production does not require prisma seed. Use matching **SERVER_PORT** and **COLLECTOR_PORT** integers in both child environments, NODE_ENV=production, **LLM_PROVIDER=openai** and fake **OPEN_AI_KEY**. Leave OPEN_MODEL_PREF unset to retain the native agent fallback **gpt-5.6-luna** / Responses API; leave EMBEDDING_ENGINE unset for the native local embedder and VECTOR_DB unset for native LanceDB. Start `node index.js` separately in collector cwd and server cwd under the capture environment. The server's CollectorApi always uses `http://127.0.0.1:COLLECTOR_PORT` (default8888), so no invented COLLECTOR_BASE_URL is needed. Server health is GET `/api/ping`200 at SERVER_PORT (default3001). [SQLite datasource](https://github.com/Mintplex-Labs/anything-llm/blob/eb498cf2c2111b0c651d4c3781f0cdfb8168935c/server/prisma/schema.prisma#L13), [production migration/startup](https://github.com/Mintplex-Labs/anything-llm/blob/eb498cf2c2111b0c651d4c3781f0cdfb8168935c/docker/docker-entrypoint.sh), [actual collector address](https://github.com/Mintplex-Labs/anything-llm/blob/eb498cf2c2111b0c651d4c3781f0cdfb8168935c/server/utils/collectorApi/index.js#L39), [native model fallback](https://github.com/Mintplex-Labs/anything-llm/blob/eb498cf2c2111b0c651d4c3781f0cdfb8168935c/server/utils/agents/index.js#L330), [native Responses provider](https://github.com/Mintplex-Labs/anything-llm/blob/eb498cf2c2111b0c651d4c3781f0cdfb8168935c/server/utils/agents/aibitat/providers/openai.js).

Public embedder assets can be loaded outside tap through the **real NativeEmbedder.embedderClient()** method, not a fake model or replacement encoder. From server cwd with the same private STORAGE_DIR, run `node -e 'const {NativeEmbedder}=require("./utils/EmbeddingEngines/native"); new NativeEmbedder().embedderClient().then(()=>process.exit(0)).catch(e=>{console.error(e);process.exit(1)})'`. This downloads/loads the declared native Xenova/all-MiniLM-L6-v2 pipeline into STORAGE_DIR/models, using HF and the native Mintplex CDN fallback if necessary. It makes no remote model API call. Ensure the storage directory exists first and retain these public assets for actual/fresh startup. LanceDB uses STORAGE_DIR/lancedb. The server's tiktoken helper uses bundled js-tiktoken, rather than an external tiktoken asset fetch. [Native asset-loading method](https://github.com/Mintplex-Labs/anything-llm/blob/eb498cf2c2111b0c651d4c3781f0cdfb8168935c/server/utils/EmbeddingEngines/native/index.js), [native LanceDB directory](https://github.com/Mintplex-Labs/anything-llm/blob/eb498cf2c2111b0c651d4c3781f0cdfb8168935c/server/utils/vectorDbProviders/lance/index.js#L21), [bundled tokenizer](https://github.com/Mintplex-Labs/anything-llm/blob/eb498cf2c2111b0c651d4c3781f0cdfb8168935c/server/utils/helpers/tiktoken.js).

On a fresh owned instance with native single-user auth defaults (AUTH_TOKEN/JWT_SECRET unset), use the normal API key creation route; do not inject a test key into database tables:

| Step | Exact native request/response |
| --- | --- |
| API key | POST `/api/system/generate-api-key` `{name:"Phistory"}` → `{apiKey:{secret:KEY,...},error:null}`. The Bearer value is **apiKey.secret**, not a top-level token. |
| Workspace | Bearer POST `/api/v1/workspace/new` `{name:"Phistory",chatMode:"chat"}` → `{workspace:{slug:SLUG,...},message:...}`. Preserve the native prompt/tool configuration; no custom system text is needed. |
| Actual task | Bearer POST `/api/v1/workspace/SLUG/chat` `{message:"@agent "+TEXT,mode:"chat"}`. Optional sessionId may be omitted. This invokes real EphemeralAgentHandler → init/createAIbitat/startAgentCluster, not the ordinary untooled chat lane or a caller-built agent. |

The fresh native system begins `Given the following conversation, relevant context, and a follow up question,` and expands its native datetime variable. This prefix alone also exists in ordinary chat, so require the real **@agent task path, task text and positive native tool definitions** to identify the primary request. Default skills are native memory, document summarizer, web scraping and web browsing; preserve all registered definitions even when the simple task invokes none. Registration does not require a browser launch. No custom MCP, imported skill, document or remote account is required for this fresh workspace. [Key creation](https://github.com/Mintplex-Labs/anything-llm/blob/eb498cf2c2111b0c651d4c3781f0cdfb8168935c/server/endpoints/system.js#L1064), [REST workspace/chat schema](https://github.com/Mintplex-Labs/anything-llm/blob/eb498cf2c2111b0c651d4c3781f0cdfb8168935c/server/endpoints/api/workspace/index.js), [native REST agent dispatch](https://github.com/Mintplex-Labs/anything-llm/blob/eb498cf2c2111b0c651d4c3781f0cdfb8168935c/server/utils/chats/apiChatHandler.js#L191), [real agent cluster](https://github.com/Mintplex-Labs/anything-llm/blob/eb498cf2c2111b0c651d4c3781f0cdfb8168935c/server/utils/agents/ephemeral.js#L529), [default system](https://github.com/Mintplex-Labs/anything-llm/blob/eb498cf2c2111b0c651d4c3781f0cdfb8168935c/server/models/systemSettings.js#L39), [default native skills](https://github.com/Mintplex-Labs/anything-llm/blob/eb498cf2c2111b0c651d4c3781f0cdfb8168935c/server/utils/agents/defaults.js#L9).

Exact source cache: `/private/tmp/phistory-batch10-contracts/anything-llm--*`, including Docker/package manifests, schema/entrypoint, both real service entries, validatedRequest, key/workspace/chat routes, API agent handler/defaults/provider, NativeEmbedder and LanceDB. ### AgentLink CLI 0.65.1: source build and native PTY composer

Current fixed source is `reefbarman/agentlink@5e6450f0a8f420991ddff5c4c17f921c5f40790c`. Its extension/root version1.94.1 is **not** the CLI version: apps/cli/package.json is **@agentlink/cli0.65.1, private:true**, Node>=22.19.0. Both public npm latest endpoints `@agentlink/cli` and `agentlink` return404; the documented packaged preview is Darwin ARM64 only. A public Linux npm install is therefore not the current viable channel. Build the actual fixed-source workspace with `npm ci` and `npm run build:workspaces`; this is the official complete private-workspace dependency closure and includes apps/cli. `npm run cli:build` alone assumes its sibling compiled packages already exist. Run `node apps/cli/dist/agentlink.js`, preserving the compiled package bundle instead of copying only its entry file. [Actual CLI package](https://github.com/reefbarman/agentlink/blob/5e6450f0a8f420991ddff5c4c17f921c5f40790c/apps/cli/package.json), [workspace build scripts](https://github.com/reefbarman/agentlink/blob/5e6450f0a8f420991ddff5c4c17f921c5f40790c/package.json), [CLI channel documentation](https://github.com/reefbarman/agentlink/blob/5e6450f0a8f420991ddff5c4c17f921c5f40790c/apps/cli/README.md).

Set private **AGENTLINK_HOME**, a real private project path and fake **AGENTLINK_OPENAI_API_KEY** (or OPENAI_API_KEY). Before capture, configure the normal direct OpenAI model lane with `node apps/cli/dist/agentlink.js --project WORKDIR config model openai/gpt-5.6-sol`; this persists config.defaultModel plus the native Code mode preference, and prints `Default model: openai/gpt-5.6-sol`. It does not make a model validation call. The normal default is Codex OAuth; this explicitly named direct-OpenAI lane avoids a real OAuth account and Linux keyring dependency, rather than pretending to capture an authenticated Codex session. [Native config action](https://github.com/reefbarman/agentlink/blob/5e6450f0a8f420991ddff5c4c17f921c5f40790c/apps/cli/src/cli.ts#L409), [normal environment credential resolution](https://github.com/reefbarman/agentlink/blob/5e6450f0a8f420991ddff5c4c17f921c5f40790c/apps/cli/src/credentials.ts).

The actual chat command **requires TTY input and output**. Spawn `node apps/cli/dist/agentlink.js --project WORKDIR chat` in a real PTY with TERM=xterm-256color and ordinary100x30 dimensions. Remove CI from this child environment, as the official TUI smoke does, while retaining the capture proxy/fake credentials and private data paths. NO_COLOR=1 and FORCE_COLOR=0 are supported for simpler diagnostics. Wait with an overall deadline for the ANSI-stripped native composer placeholder **`What would you like AgentLink to work on?`**, then write the single-line task and a **carriage return `\r`** to that same PTY. TextArea handles an unmodified Return as onSubmit; Ctrl+J inserts a newline and is not submission. The Wordmark alone is not readiness. [TTY requirement/native host construction](https://github.com/reefbarman/agentlink/blob/5e6450f0a8f420991ddff5c4c17f921c5f40790c/apps/cli/src/cli.ts#L403), [official PTY readiness recipe](https://github.com/reefbarman/agentlink/blob/5e6450f0a8f420991ddff5c4c17f921c5f40790c/apps/cli/smoke-tui.mjs), [actual idle composer](https://github.com/reefbarman/agentlink/blob/5e6450f0a8f420991ddff5c4c17f921c5f40790c/apps/cli/src/tui/InkChatApp.tsx#L700), [Return versus newline](https://github.com/reefbarman/agentlink/blob/5e6450f0a8f420991ddff5c4c17f921c5f40790c/apps/cli/src/tui/TextArea.tsx#L64).

Use that native chat host with sharedPromptGuidance=true, actual workspace indexing and native tool registration. The official smoke script is a readiness/interaction reference only: it exercises UI controls and does not itself submit a model task. Do not replace the real WorkspaceHost with a mocked controller/projection or borrow the VS Code extension's system identity for the CLI. Validate the complete actual direct-OpenAI task body/tools; retain native Code-mode/shared guidance and any real workspace-dependent prompt text. No browser or desktop is needed. ## Native Electron application contracts

These are **the applications' own Electron runtimes and real renderer windows** under Xvfb. Playwright's Electron connection attaches to that native application; it does not launch a substitute Chrome/Firefox/Chromium browser. An additional external-browser OAuth/navigation task remains outside this contract; ordinary BYOK avoids it. Both Orkas and Dyad now have independently verified real Linux captures at their named native entry points.

### Orkas real Electron Linux runtime

**Verified initial Commander:** [initial and independent fresh44153049669](https://github.com/DragonnZhang/phistory/actions/runs/44153049669) preserve native application2026.11.16/Electron42.11.18/Node24.19.0 sidecars and configured custom gpt-4.1. Its20 complete JSON tool definitions are exactly equal in original order: read_files, bash, process_session, list_files, web_fetch, web_search, cross_session_memory, publish_outputs, search_files, grep_files, chat_history, tool_result, inner_browser, skill_search, dispatch_to, hand_off_to, run_worker, open_app_view, tool_load and run_program. These are all schemas **actually emitted in the initial request**. No claim is made that every tool_load-activated group was already expanded. The42,959-character raw systems differ on only four lines: builtin/platform/custom Agent-root paths and the actual userWorkSpace task cwd/default-write path. Normalizing only the actual temporary /tmp/phistory-home-* prefix and32-character native local uid yields exactly equal system strings; user messages, tool arrays and every other request-body field match without modification. Raw traces retain their original values.

Current official HEAD is **3163c7ef1c2b03e21e6f85c6301515609361c570**, package/release2026.11.16. The manifest owns Electron42.11.18 and npm11.11.0. Unlike the earlier generic account concern, **the open-source app does not need an online Orkas login**: real boot calls `users.initActiveUser({openSource:true})`; an empty private workspace creates its own32-character local uid and normal open-users.json, activates the cloud/local subtree and native auth store. Do not copy the fixture's hard-coded account-e2e identity or seed an invented authenticated account. [Native first-boot identity](https://github.com/Orkas-AI/Orkas/blob/3163c7ef1c2b03e21e6f85c6301515609361c570/src/main/features/users.ts), [actual boot](https://github.com/Orkas-AI/Orkas/blob/3163c7ef1c2b03e21e6f85c6301515609361c570/src/main/index.ts#L747), [manifest](https://github.com/Orkas-AI/Orkas/blob/3163c7ef1c2b03e21e6f85c6301515609361c570/package.json).

**Official Ubuntu source support is explicit.** The upstream linux-source-smoke workflow uses Ubuntu22.04 x64/arm64, Node24, npm ci, Xvfb/D-Bus and a genuine app/renderer/preload smoke. Follow its standard dependency preparation, rather than replacing modules or stubbing native gates:

```sh
npm ci
node scripts/ensure-dev-dependencies.cjs
dbus-run-session -- xvfb-run --auto-servernum node CAPTURE_DRIVER.cjs
```

Install normal Electron Linux runtime libraries and the upstream desktop packages xvfb, openbox, x11-utils, dbus and ripgrep. Its source gate requires glibc, supports x64/arm64 and rejects musl. The complete preparation script verifies/rebuilds native SQLite ABI and provisions real bundled Node/Python/uv, embedding weights, OfficeCLI, FFmpeg and supported Whisper/LibRaw assets, plus normal notification/passkey helpers. npm ci itself runs the declared SQLite/embedding postinstall. Prewarm these public packages/assets outside tap; retain all gate failures rather than substituting fake inference/native libraries. The official standalone linux-electron-smoke script only renders a data URL and is **not** sufficient as a harness capture.
